The heading check moves to this ADR and the earlier series checks are preserved rather than replaced.
- **The accessibility family is named `2N-ACCESS`**, for the same governance reason ADR-102 and ADR-104 recorded: a family name containing digits does not match `2N-[A-Z]+-\d{3}` — the shape both the phase-start detector and the traceability generator use — which is how Phase 2K's seven accessibility requirements became invisible to every prose count.
- **Consequences.** The `2N-*` requirement namespace becomes declarable, which is why the retarget is mandatory rather than tidy. `docs/initiatives/phase-2n/` and `docs/reports/phase-2n/` are created with their governing artifacts and `docs/reports/README.md` marks `phase-2n/` as the active initiative, **planning only**. The planning pass adds **no RLS policy, no grant, no role, no secret, no external service, no second write path, no new AI operation kind and no service-role client**, and performs **no provider call at all**, so the usage ledger is untouched. **ADR-055's expiry of 2026-10-27 is unchanged by this authorization** — this phase builds no retrieval widening and feeds no funnel — and the closeout family requires it restated as neither satisfied nor superseded. `2E-COMMAND-012` remains deferred behind ADR-057's unexecuted reopening gate and is **not** pulled in. **The signup rollout gate is untouched** and Phase 2N must not be read as progress toward it. **Per-slice acceptance records, the traceability matrix, the closing report and any deployment record are forbidden until their gates**, and this planning pass creates none of them.
- **Superseded in part by ADR-109 (2026-08-12), which signs all seventeen decisions and raises the budget from a proposed two to three allocated.** This ADR is **not rewritten**: the ceiling of four, the seventeen open questions, the options considered and the recommendations it carried are the record of what was decided on 2026-08-12 before the owner answered, and they stand. ADR-109 layers on top. **Implementation remains unauthorized by both.**

## ADR-109 — The owner signs all seventeen Phase 2N decisions and fixes a non-transferable three-migration budget, without authorizing implementation

- **Date:** 2026-08-12
- **Status:** Accepted — an **owner decision**, taken after reviewing the complete Phase 2N planning package delivered as draft PR #191. It **discharges the seventeen decision gates** and fixes the migration budget. It authorizes **no implementation**: no product code, no page, no component, **no migration**, no schema, RLS, grant, policy or RPC change, no Edge Function, no deploy, no secret, no external integration, no resumption of the push investigation, no repair of the timezone defects, no merging of PR #191, and no successor phase. The three migrations below are **destinations, not permissions** — none may be created until implementation is separately authorized.
- **Context.** The package was delivered at `56b5d2e` with CI green 3/3, against `main` at `eab6d08`, **92 migrations at hosted parity `202608120092`**, signup closed and the rollout gate at **25 pass · 3 fail · 2 owner-signature**. The audit's eight corrections stand unchallenged and are the basis of several signatures below.
- **Decision 1 — OD-2N-1 is signed as option A: identity stays name-uniqueness this phase, and `entity_aliases` gains its first reader.** No canonical-identity pointer, no merge follows, **no migration**. A nickname resolves to the person it names. This switches on a mechanism the repository built, granted and never used — the audit found `entity_aliases` with **zero readers and zero writers** — which is why A is the cheapest honest answer rather than the most conservative one. Option B (a canonical pointer) is declined for this phase; it is not refused in principle.
- **Decision 2 — OD-2N-2 is signed as option A: projects follow the same identity principle as people.** Two identity models in one product is the divergence this phase exists to remove. Aliases may be consumed for projects where it makes sense. **No migration.**
- **Decision 3 — OD-2N-3 is signed as option A: no merge in Phase 2N.** No automatic suggestion, no merge RPC, no silent relinking of relations. **The corresponding requirements close `not-built-by-rule` against this signature, never as `partial`** — nothing of merge is being built, and a partial with an invented remainder is exactly the shape the traceability contract refuses.
- **Decision 4 — OD-2N-4 is signed as option A, conditionally and for a future phase.** If merge is ever authorized it **must** be reversible, with a complete preview, explicit confirmation, a registered undo and a proof against a populated fixture. Recorded now so a later phase inherits the contract rather than re-deciding it under pressure. **Nothing of merge is implemented in Phase 2N.**
- **Decision 5 — OD-2N-5 is signed as option A: memory correction reuses the existing authority paths** — `updateMemory`, `setMemoryLifecycle` and the paths already in place. **No second correction path and no supersession RPC this phase. No migration.**
- **Decision 6 — OD-2N-6 is signed as option A: `active` and `archived`, and archiving must actually mean something.** No `suppressed` column. **An archived memory must genuinely stop participating in retrieval** — hiding it at presentation is explicitly refused, which is what makes this signature cost migration **M1**. Archive is **not** a hard delete and must never be described as one, and **correcting stays distinct from archiving**.
- **Decision 7 — OD-2N-7 is signed as option A: conflicts are derived at read time from existing data.** Both claims, both sources, both validity windows, **no silent choice**, no conflict table and no persisted conflict lifecycle. Where an action exists, the conflict routes into "Precisa de você". **No migration.**
- **Decision 8 — OD-2N-8 is signed as option A: only owner-authored relations persist.** Extraction may produce a **proposal**, and a proposal is not a relation. No inferred relation is persisted automatically. **Existing rows are presented as owner-authored without inventing retroactive provenance** — the honest reading, since the product cannot now discover where they came from. **No relation-provenance migration.**
- **Decision 9 — OD-2N-9 is signed as option B: a genuinely more useful library.** Files linked to people and projects and back again, provenance, processing states, failure recovery, classification, filters and richer discovery, with sensitivity handled throughout. **No new migration** — `entity_attachments` already exists, is owner-scoped and is trigger-validated — and if the re-audit proves a material need incompatible with the budget, that is a **stop condition and an owner decision**, never a quiet reallocation. The library must not become a second storage system or a duplicate of global search.
- **Decision 10 — OD-2N-10 is signed as option B: a secondary relationship graph, under a contract that can refuse it.** It is never primary navigation; it uses only authorized persisted relations, so **under OD-2N-8 A no unconfirmed inferred relation can appear in it**; every edge is explainable, with origin *"informed by you"* where that is the only truth available; no numeric confidence is rendered as certainty; **no meaning is attributed to position, distance, cluster or centrality**; and a complete text/list alternative exists that is keyboard- and screen-reader-accessible and **is not a degraded version**. **No additional migration.** If the graph cannot satisfy these contracts within the budget, the work **stops and proposes a reduction** rather than shipping a decorative or misleading version.
- **Decision 11 — OD-2N-11 is signed as option B: transactional deletion of a person, a project and a memory.** One validated owner-scoped authority path; a preview enumerating consequences; explicit confirmation; **enumerated propagation**; removal from retrieval; defined treatment of relations, associations, linked files, tasks and memories; audit; and a registered undo or compensation. **No client-side multi-delete, no partial deletion, and no soft delete presented as removal.** Migration **M3** is exclusive to it. The audit must confirm the consequences **per type** before implementation, and **a propagation that cannot be truthfully undone is a stop condition**, not a smaller undo.
- **Decision 12 — OD-2N-12 is signed as option A: the contextual surfaces join the sensitivity contract.** Person, project, memory, file, and relation/graph wherever it renders information derived from those sources. Classification is **derived from the source**, **never inferred as `normal` by absence** — an unreadable, foreign or missing source resolves to the most protective case. Content is **masked in position**, revealed locally and explicitly; **counts stay true**; sensitive rows are not dropped from a list to simplify it. **No new persistence and no migration.** This signature **blocks any contextual page that keeps rendering raw content outside `GOVERNED_SURFACES`**.
- **Decision 13 — OD-2N-13 is signed as option B: a separate timezone initiative, before slice 2N.1.** Phase 2N enumerates the defects, preserves the evidence of **13 call sites across 12 files** (or the count the current audit re-derives), prevents its own code from introducing a new zone-less formatter, and has **2N.0 guard only its own surfaces**. The initiative is a **mandatory dependency of 2N.1** and may be planned or executed **only under separate authorization**. **The ~27 call sites are not absorbed into 2N.0**, and no timezone repair happens in the planning PR.
- **Decision 14 — OD-2N-14 is signed as option B: `3 allocated · obligation ZERO · NON-TRANSFERABLE`.** **M1** — slice 2N.3 — makes retrieval respect validity **before the bound**. **M2** — slice 2N.7 — carries the content-free telemetry vocabulary, **only if real producers and consumers are specified and delivered**. **M3** — slice 2N.3 — carries transactional deletion and its propagation. **No migration for adjustments; none is transferable; none may carry another's responsibility; a fourth is a STOP CONDITION.** *Obligation zero* means an allocation may close **unspent** if the re-audit proves it unnecessary — an unspent allocation is not a defect, and a migration created to use up an allocation fails the close. **None is created during planning.**
- **Decision 15 — OD-2N-15 is signed as option A: a small content-free event set.** Before **M2** exists, each event carries a product question, a producer, a consumer, a surface, closed properties, a justification, a forbidden-content test, a planned hosted proof, and a cleanup and zero-residue proof. **No name, title, memory content, filename, relation, person, project, conflict text, raw error, or identifier that functions as content** may be recorded. M2 is exclusive to this delivery.
- **Decision 16 — OD-2N-16 is signed as option A: no Phase 2N requirement depends on real hardware.** Mobile is proved in viewports and browsers. The phase **does not inherit the push checkpoint**. Android stays **NOT EXECUTED** in its own initiative and push stays a **parallel residual**. **No real VoiceOver or TalkBack run may be declared as executed**, and the absence of hardware **does not block Phase 2N's closeout**.
- **Decision 17 — OD-2N-17 is signed as option A: ADR-055 is untouched.** Phase 2N does not satisfy it, does not supersede it, does not widen semantic retrieval, and does not feed its funnel as though that were progress. **The expiry of 2026-10-27 stands**, and it must be re-evaluated at the correct gate if that date is reached.
- **What the signatures changed in the package, stated rather than smoothed.** The plan **recommended a budget of two** and the owner signed **three**, funding deletion — which the gaps report ranked as the single largest improvement in user control available in this phase. The plan **recommended declining the graph** and the owner **authorized it under a contract that can refuse it**, which is a different and stronger answer than either building it unconditionally or not at all. The plan **recommended the smaller library** and the owner signed the larger one **without funding a migration for it**, which is coherent only because `entity_attachments` already exists — and the plan now proves that, per capability, rather than asserting it.
- **The requirement count moves from 108 to 123, and no id was renumbered, reused or removed.** Fifteen identifiers are **appended to the end of their existing families** to carry obligations the signatures created — deletion's preview, enumeration, eviction, undo and stop condition; the library's back-links, classification, filters and boundary; the graph's explainability, meaning-free layout and refusal clause; alias authorship; the timezone initiative as a dependency; and telemetry's zero-residue proof. Twelve existing requirements are **restated to what was signed** rather than added to — narrowing an unsigned requirement to its signature is not a scope change, while inventing a new id for the same obligation would have been.
- **Alternatives considered.** *Fold the signatures into ADR-108 by editing it* (rejected: an accepted ADR is not rewritten, and the options the owner declined are part of the record — a reader must be able to see that a canonical-identity pointer, a merge, a persisted conflict lifecycle and a relation-provenance migration were each considered and declined, and why). *Reallocate M2 to the library when option B was signed* (rejected: a transferable budget is not a budget; the plan instead proves the library needs no schema, and a proven need would be a stop condition). *Classify the merge requirements `partial`* (rejected by the owner's own signature: nothing of merge is being built, and a partial with no remainder is the shape the contract refuses). *Absorb the timezone repair into 2N.0 now that its guard extension is planned anyway* (rejected: OD-2N-13 B exists precisely to stop a foundation slice acquiring ~27 call sites on surfaces this phase does not otherwise touch). *Treat the graph authorization as permission to ship something* (rejected: OD-2N-10 B is a contract with a refusal clause, and a decorative graph would fail it).
- **Consequences.** The seventeen gates are discharged and **implementation is still not authorized** — a separate owner decision is required before any slice runs. The budget is fixed at **`3 allocated · 0 spent`**, non-transferable, with a fourth as a stop condition, and **no migration exists**. The estimate and the critical path are revised to carry deletion and the enlarged library, and the timezone initiative is recorded as a **mandatory, separately authorized dependency of 2N.1**. Push stays a parallel residual — **implemented and hosted, failing on a real iPhone with HTTP 403, cause unproven, Android NOT EXECUTED** — and no Phase 2N requirement may depend on it. **Signup stays closed, the rollout gate stays untouched at 25 · 3 · 2, PR #191 stays a draft, and A13 continues to target the roadmap successor, which this ADR does not authorize, does not scope and does not name in its heading.**
- **Amended by ADR-110 (2026-08-12), which confirms the `2N-PRIVACY-007` interpretation and fixes the posture of `people.notes`.** This ADR is **not rewritten**: it left that reading flagged rather than settled, which is the record of what was true when it was signed. ADR-110 settles it, adds no migration, and does not change any of the seventeen signatures.

## ADR-110 — The owner confirms the field-classification reading and fixes the posture of free-text notes, adding no migration

- **Date:** 2026-08-12
- **Status:** Accepted — an **owner decision**, taken on the one interpretation ADR-109 deliberately left flagged. It settles `2N-PRIVACY-007`, fixes a posture for `people.notes`, and **authorizes no implementation, no migration, no column, no schema change and no new authority**. It does not alter any of the seventeen signatures and does not change the budget.
- **Context.** `OD-2N-12` signed a fail-closed rule: classification derives from the source, and an unreadable, foreign or missing source resolves to the most protective case. Read literally over *every* field, that would mask a person's own **name** on their own page — a name has no source entry — which makes the page unusable and was plainly not the intent. The planning package refused to resolve this silently and recorded it at `2N-PRIVACY-007` as an interpretation requiring confirmation. This ADR is that confirmation, and it goes further than the question asked.
- **Decision 1 — the fail-closed rule governs source-derived content.** It applies to content whose classification derives from a source record: entry text, task titles, memory bodies, file names and extracted text. It does **not** automatically mask a **structural field the owner typed directly onto the entity**.
- **Decision 2 — a person's name and a project's name stay visible on their own contextual page.** A **structural identifier needed to recognise the entity** is not treated as unsourced derived content. Counts and the entity's existence stay true.
- **Decision 3 — and this does not make every owner-typed field `normal`.** The distinction is **not** owner-authored versus derived. It is **structural identifier versus free text**, and the owner drew it explicitly rather than letting Decision 1 be generalised into a licence.
- **Decision 4 — `people.notes` is masked by default on contextual surfaces.** It is free text about a human being, it carries **no classification of its own** and **no classifiable source**, and it is exactly the field a fail-open reading would expose. Reveal is **local, explicit and accessible**. **Absence of classification never resolves to `normal`.**
- **Decision 5 — `people.notes` is not displayed in full anywhere indirect**: not in search, suggestions, previews, related pages, the graph, telemetry, or any indirect contextual surface. **The person's name and aliases stay searchable**, and the person's existence and structural counts stay true — masking the note is not hiding the person.
- **Decision 6 — while it carries no reliable classification, `people.notes` is not content the Brain retrieves.** The audit's verification, run before this ADR: `match_internal_knowledge` unions `entry_embeddings` and `memories` only and **never reads `people`**, and no chat or conversation-source path reads `notes`. **The property is already true today**; this decision makes it a stated obligation with a guard rather than an accident of implementation.
- **Decision 7 — what is forbidden.** **No `sensitivity` column** on `people`, and **no migration** for any of this. **No sensitivity may be inferred from the text of a note.** **No existing note may be deleted or altered by this phase.**
- **Decision 8 — the stop condition.** If removing `people.notes` from retrieval or from search turns out to require a migration, a new column or an authority not already present, **work stops and returns to the owner**. It may **not** consume or reallocate **M1**, **M2** or **M3**.
- **Why this needs no migration, verified rather than asserted.** Search's `people` domain reads `columns: ["name", "notes"]` with `snippetColumn: "notes"` from `DOMAIN_SPECS`, a **TypeScript constant**; narrowing it is a code change. The sensitivity contract's rules are **data in a module**. Retrieval never reaches `people` at all. `src/lib/closeout/phase-2i-search-guard.test.ts` does not pin `notes` into the people domain, so the narrowing breaks no existing guard. **Nothing in this decision reaches the database.**
- **One consequence recorded rather than left to be discovered: this narrows behaviour ADR-093 signed.** ADR-093 fixed Phase 2I's search decisions, including which columns each domain matches and snippets. Removing `notes` from the `people` domain is a **deliberate, owner-signed narrowing**, not an accidental reopening — `2N-PRIVACY-006` requires exactly that distinction to be stated, and it is stated here. **ADR-093's default exclusion of `highly_sensitive` is untouched**, and no other domain changes.
- **The requirement count moves from 123 to 127, and no id was renumbered, reused or removed.** Four identifiers are **appended to the end of the `2N-PRIVACY` family**: the field taxonomy, the notes posture, the indirect-surface and retrieval prohibition, and the acceptance that proves all of it.
- **Alternatives considered.** *Leave `people.notes` shown, since the owner typed it* (rejected: it is the single most likely place in the product for something genuinely private about a named human being, and it has no classification to fall back on). *Add a `sensitivity` column to `people`* (rejected: it would be a fourth migration, it contradicts `2N-PRIVACY-003`, and it repeats the persisted-classification move `OD-2L-1` B refused for tasks). *Infer a level from the note's text* (rejected: an AI-derived classification presented as a protection is a guess wearing a lock, and it would be the first time this product classified content without a user act). *Mask the name too, for consistency with the literal rule* (rejected by Decision 2: it makes the page unusable and protects nothing, since the name is how the user found the page). *Delete or rewrite existing notes* (rejected: destroying user data to simplify a classification problem).
- **Consequences.** `2N-PRIVACY-007` becomes unambiguous and four requirements are added. The 2N.0 slice gains the notes posture and its acceptance; the threat model gains the unclassifiable-free-text threat; the traceability contract gains refusals for a fail-open default, an inferred classification and an indirect leak. **The budget is unchanged at `3 allocated · 0 spent · none created`, and no fourth allocation is opened.** Implementation remains unauthorized, **the planning package may be merged**, and merging it authorizes nothing further. Signup stays closed, the rollout gate stays at 25 · 3 · 2, push is not resumed, Android stays **NOT EXECUTED**, the timezone initiative stays separate and unauthorized, and A13 continues to target the roadmap successor, which this ADR does not authorize, does not scope and does not name in its heading.

## ADR-111 — The owner authorizes the Local Day Correction initiative through closeout, with a zero-migration budget

- **Date:** 2026-08-12
- **Status:** Accepted — an **owner decision**. It authorizes a compact initiative end to end: governance, planning, implementation, tests, browser journeys, guards, branches, pull requests, merges, CI on the exact merge SHAs, an ordinary application deployment, authenticated production verification, and closeout. It authorizes **no migration and no schema change of any kind**, and it does **not** authorize Phase 2N implementation or Phase 2O.
- **Context.** The merged Phase 2M successor re-audit found **seventeen call sites in sixteen files** rendering a stored instant with **no timezone**, so they show the host's day — UTC on a server — rather than the owner's. Four of them were already recorded by `2M-TIME-007` as `HOST_ZONE_FORMATTERS_CARRIED_PAST_CLOSE` and deferred honestly. **Thirteen were not deferred; they were invisible**, because the guard that would have caught them names eight directories and none of the thirteen is in one. `docs/STATE.md` already named this work "the separate timezone initiative" and made it **a mandatory dependency of slice 2N.1**. This ADR authorizes it, and nothing beyond it.
- **Decision 1 — the local day has one definition and it is already written.** `src/lib/time/local-day.ts` (`2M-TIME-001`/`-002`/`-003`) and `src/lib/time/instant-format.ts` (`2M-TIME-005`/`-006`) are the contract. This initiative **reuses** them and does not restate, fork or re-derive them on any surface.
- **Decision 2 — the zone comes from the owner's declared preference, resolved once.** `profiles.timezone`, through a single `resolveOwnerTimeZone`. **Four private answers** to "is this zone usable" existed outside the contract and become one: the **three byte-identical `isValidTimeZone` predicates** in `operations/actions.ts`, `task-commands/actions.ts` and `task-commands/detail-actions.ts`, **and `resolveProfileTimezone` in `daily-cycle/review-projection.ts`** — which is not a clone but a *resolver* carrying `"America/Sao_Paulo"` as a literal rather than reading `defaultAgentPreferences`, and is the loosest of the four. **A census of formatters would have walked straight past it**, which is the argument for making the consolidation mechanical: `DUPLICATE_ZONE_RESOLVERS` is enumerated tree-wide, refuses a fifth, and each row must still exist until Unit 4 deletes it. The resolver is deliberately **stricter** than the copies it replaces — a bare `EST` carries no DST rule and is a fixed offset wearing a zone's name — and this narrows nothing reachable, because `profileSchema.ianaTimezone` already refuses to store one.
- **Decision 3 — "today" is computed, not merely formatted.** The Home dashboard is the case that makes this a semantic decision rather than a cosmetic one: its header formats `new Date()` with no zone while the task list fifteen lines above uses `workProjection.timezone`. Between 21:00 and midnight in `America/Sao_Paulo` one screen names two days.
- **Decision 4 — the guard is built and proved before a single surface is repaired.** Tree-wide over `src/`, four families, every current occurrence transcribed with an **exact count**, asserted in both directions so a repaired file fails until its row is deleted and a newly-broken exempt file fails too. Mutation controls per family, plus a real planted defect proved to fail by name and line. **An occurrence is never closed by editing the detector.**
- **Decision 5 — the census governs the scope, not the sentence "seventeen".** The detector found three further families the phase corpus never looked for: seven host-zone field operations computing the review period in `generateReview`, four UTC day slices, and three zone round-trips. **Thirty-one occurrences total**, all classified individually, all corrected. Fifteen occurrences of a fixed 24 hours are **durations, not day boundaries**, and are correctly left alone.
- **Decision 6 — one behaviour change is intended and is stated rather than discovered.** `generateReview` currently computes `daily`, `weekly` and `monthly` periods in the host's zone and stores UTC date labels. After this initiative it computes them in the owner's zone. A review generated at 22:00 in São Paulo will cover a different period than it did before, **because the previous one was wrong**. **No stored summary is rewritten, reprocessed or back-dated.**
- **Decision 7 — what is forbidden.** No migration, column, schema, RLS, grant, policy or RPC change. No new timezone preference. No change to the meaning of `planned_at`. No date library. No browser zone as authority on a server path. No fix that corrects the text while leaving the filter or the ordering wrong. No closing an occurrence by removing it from the detector. No hardware pass claimed. No resumption of push or notifications. No Phase 2N implementation and no new 2N requirement. Signup stays closed and the rollout gate stays at 25 · 3 · 2.
- **Decision 8 — the stop condition.** If any correction turns out to need a migration, a column, a schema change, persistent reprocessing, or an amendment to a signed Phase 2N contract, **work stops and returns to the owner**. It may not consume or reallocate **M1**, **M2** or **M3**, which stay allocated to Phase 2N and non-transferable.
- **Why this needs no migration, verified rather than asserted.** Every one of the thirty-one occurrences is at the **read, render or compute boundary**. `profiles.timezone` already exists and is already selected by most of the affected surfaces. The contract modules are pure — `instant-format.ts` has no writer, asserted by its own test. Nothing here reads a new column, writes a value, or reprocesses stored data.
- **Alternatives considered.** *Fold the repair into Phase 2N slice 2N.1, which rebuilds these pages anyway* (rejected: it makes a live defect wait on an unauthorized phase, and it would mix a mechanical correction into a phase whose diffs need to stay reviewable). *Repair the seventeen and leave the three further families* (rejected: the review-period computation is the same defect and is materially worse, and leaving `utc-day-slice` non-empty would require a standing allowlist a later author could widen). *Widen `2M-TIME-007`'s corpus instead of adding a guard* (rejected: that guard's named corpus is the executable form of a requirement that asked for the surfaces to be named; broadening it would falsify the requirement rather than satisfy a new one). *Default the zone inside `local-day.ts` so callers cannot forget it* (rejected: the contract's refusal to invent a zone is the property that makes it trustworthy at 23:00; the fix is a total resolver at the edge, not a default at the centre). *Add a timezone preference distinct from `profiles.timezone`* (rejected: explicitly forbidden, and a second authority is the defect, not the cure).
- **Consequences.** The initiative gets `docs/initiatives/local-day-correction/` and `docs/reports/local-day-correction/`. Phase 2M's `HOST_ZONE_FORMATTERS_CARRIED_PAST_CLOSE` list is **emptied and retired**, discharging a debt that phase carried past its own close. Slice 2N.1's mandatory dependency is satisfied. **Migrations stay at 92, parity stays `202608120092`, `verify:edge-parity` stays green, and zero Edge Function changes are expected.** Phase 2N stays **planned and unimplemented**, Phase 2O stays **not started**, and A13 continues to target the roadmap successor, which this ADR does not authorize, does not scope and does not name in its heading.

## ADR-112 — The owner authorizes Phase 2N implementation through closeout, and corrects the three time requirements the completed dependency made stale

- **Date:** 2026-08-13
- **Status:** Accepted — an **owner decision**. It authorizes **implementation through closeout** of Phase 2N: product code, pages, components, Server Actions, tests, browser journeys, guards, branches, pull requests, merges, CI on the exact merge SHAs, ordinary application deployment, authenticated hosted verification, per-slice acceptance records, the traceability matrix, the closing report and a deployment record. It authorizes **at most the three migrations already allocated**, and it authorizes **nothing else**: no fourth migration, no successor phase, no resumption of the push investigation, no opening of signup, no change to the rollout gate, no entity merge, no persisted graph inference, and no paid provider spend.
- **Context.** The dependency that gated this phase is finished. **The Local Day Correction initiative CONCLUDED on 2026-08-13** at `d581e43`, ADR-111, **zero migrations created and zero spent** — thirty-one occurrences repaired across four families, all at zero tree-wide, proved rendering in two zones on different calendar dates at one instant, in both hemispheres across a DST transition, on the deployed application, with zero fixture residue. `main` is `d581e43`, clean and synchronized; PR #198 merged at `005c42e` and PR #199 at `d581e43`, **each with CI green on its exact merge SHA**; no open PR. **92 migrations, hosted parity `202608120092`, both read live.** Signup closed, rollout **25 pass · 3 fail · 2 owner-signature**, push still failing on a real iPhone with HTTP 403 and **NOT EXECUTED** on Android.
- **The seventeen signatures are re-audited against this `main` and every one of them stands.** None is reopened, narrowed, widened or reinterpreted. `OD-2N-13` **B** is the only one whose *object* changed, and it changed by being **satisfied**: the initiative it required was authorized, executed and merged. **A decision that has been carried out is not a decision that has been altered**, which is why this ADR corrects three requirements and amends no signature.
- **Decision 1 — implementation is authorized, slice by slice, in the planned order.** `2N.0` foundations, then `2N.1` person, `2N.2` project, `2N.3` knowing/correcting/deleting, `2N.4` conflicts, `2N.5` library, `2N.6` relations and graph, `2N.7` telemetry and closeout. The order may be changed **only where a re-audit proves a real dependency**, with the reason recorded, and **a reordering may not be used to widen scope**.
- **Decision 2 — the migration budget is unchanged: `3 allocated · obligation ZERO · 0 spent · NON-TRANSFERABLE`.** **M1** validity-aware retrieval (2N.3), **M2** content-free telemetry vocabulary (2N.7, **only once real producers and consumers exist**), **M3** transactional deletion and propagation (2N.3). **A fourth is a STOP CONDITION requiring an owner decision.** No migration may be consolidated to fit the ceiling, none may be created empty or pre-emptively, an allocation may close **unspent** without that being a defect, and each spend must pass the full chain — migration chain, pgTAP, CI, dry run, byte parity, deploy and hosted proof — before it counts as delivered.
- **Decision 3 — `2N-TIME-002` is obsolete as written and is restated as `[BASELINE]`.** It obliged this phase to extend `2M-TIME-007`'s **named** corpus with Phase 2N's directories. `src/lib/closeout/local-day-correction-guard.test.ts` now takes **`src/` itself** as its corpus — 400+ files, four families, `OPEN_OCCURRENCES` empty, a per-file budget of **zero** — and Phase 2N's directories are already inside it. Adding a narrower named list beside a tree-wide rule would produce **two censuses of one defect**, and the narrower one would read as authoritative to the next author: precisely the failure `2M-TIME-007` taught and handoff §59 recorded. The restated obligation is that the tree-wide guard's four families **remain at zero after this phase's routes are added, asserted rather than assumed**. **`2N.0` builds no timezone guard of its own.**
- **Decision 4 — `2N-TIME-004` has lost its object and is restated as `[BASELINE]`.** It obliged this phase to leave the four `daily-cycle` exemptions unrepaired, re-state them as residuals, and **preserve the self-cleaning half of that exemption**. All four were repaired in the initiative's Unit 2 (`1734d34`) and `HOST_ZONE_FORMATTERS_CARRIED_PAST_CLOSE` is **empty and retired**. A slice implementing this requirement as written would be preserving a list that does not exist, and the most direct way to satisfy it literally would be to **re-create one**. The restated obligation is that the retirement holds and **no exemption list is re-created**. **This is `baseline`, not `built`: the repair was delivered by ADR-111's initiative and Phase 2N may not claim it.**
- **Decision 5 — `2N-TIME-005`'s population is corrected from 13/≈27 to 31.** The requirement's own clause — *"or the count this phase's re-audit re-derives, whichever is current"* — permits this without an amendment to any signature. The mechanically re-derived census found **17 zone-less formatters across 16 files** (not 13 across 12), plus **14** occurrences in three families the phase audit never looked for: seven host-zone field reads inside `generateReview`'s period, four UTC day slices and three zone round-trips. **Thirty-one.** The gap is instructive rather than embarrassing: an audit counting *formatters* could not see them, and the worst of them changed **what a review contained** rather than how a date was printed. The evidence is preserved in the initiative's audit and, more durably, in the guard. Restated as `[BASELINE]`.
- **Decision 6 — `2N-TIME-006` is restated for coherence with Decision 3.** Its clause *"2N.0 guards only its own surfaces"* described a guard this phase no longer builds, and its future tense described a dependency now discharged. The restatement records the dependency as **satisfied by ADR-111's initiative, concluded at `d581e43`**, and keeps its live half: **the repair was not absorbed into `2N.0` and Phase 2N may not claim it as its own delivery.** `2N-TIME-001` and `2N-TIME-003` are **unchanged** and remain obligations on code this phase has not written yet.
- **Decision 7 — the two findings the initiative's Unit 5 recorded are dispositioned, not absorbed.** (a) **`src/app/[locale]/app/loading.tsx` announces `"Carregando página"` in both locales** on a `role="status"` `aria-live="polite"` region, so a screen reader in `en` announces Portuguese. This is **inside** Phase 2N's signed scope and needs no new requirement: PRD §4 obliges every surface this phase *ships or touches* to declare its **loading** state **in both locales**, and it is the streaming fallback for all four contextual routes. It is associated with **`2N-ACCESS-005`** (both locales through a typed copy module) and **`2N-ACCESS-003`** (announced, not merely styled), and it is fixed in **`2N.0`**, where every later slice inherits it. (b) **`loadQuestionPreviews` converts a rejected row shape into an empty `Map` via `.catch(() => new Map())`**, rendering nothing while appearing to have rendered. A search of all four contextual surfaces and `src/features/entities` finds **no instance of that pattern on any Phase 2N surface**, so it is **out of scope** and is recorded as a **remainder with an explicit destination** — the questions surface, under whichever phase next opens `src/features/agent`. **It is not implemented here, and the absence of that pattern from the 2N surfaces is asserted rather than assumed.**
- **Decision 8 — what remains forbidden.** No entity merge (`OD-2N-3` A stands; those requirements close `not-built-by-rule`). No persisted inferred relation (`OD-2N-8` A). No fourth migration. No hardware claim — **a viewport is not a device and a written test is not an executed test**. No paid provider call without a separate owner decision. No opening of signup, no change to the rollout gate, no resumption of push. **No planning, starting or retargeting toward the successor phase**, which this ADR does not authorize, does not scope and does not name in its heading.
- **Decision 9 — the stop conditions that return the work to the owner.** A fourth migration; any change to a signed decision; a real provider spend; an RLS or authority change not already planned; persisted graph inference; entity merge; a material scope expansion; or a checkpoint that would depend on the owner's real hardware.
- **Traceability of this correction.** **No requirement id is renumbered, reused, deleted or silently rewritten.** Four ids are **restated in place** with an explicit `*(Restated by ADR-112: …)*` marker, following the mechanism ADR-109 used for twelve requirements and ADR-110 for one. Their prior text is preserved in this ADR and in the git history. **The requirement count stays at 127 across sixteen families**, because a restatement is not an addition — and the count is re-derived from the PRD by `phase-2n-declarations.test.ts` rather than typed.
- **Alternatives considered.** *Leave the three requirements alone and let the closeout classify them* (rejected: `2N-TIME-002` as written instructs a slice to build a second census, and a requirement that instructs the wrong work is a defect whether or not the matrix later forgives it). *Delete the three ids as obsolete* (rejected: deleting an id destroys the record that the obligation existed and was discharged, and it would break the family's gapless numbering — the exact property `phase-2n-declarations.test.ts` asserts). *Renumber the `2N-TIME` family to close the gap* (rejected outright: renumbering is the one move that makes every prior reference silently wrong). *Classify the initiative's repair as Phase 2N `built`* (rejected: it was delivered under ADR-111 by a separate initiative with its own budget and its own closeout; `baseline` is the marker this repository already has for exactly that, and claiming it would inflate this phase's delivery). *Fold the `loadQuestionPreviews` fix in while nearby* (rejected: it is on a surface this phase does not open, and "while we are here" is how a phase acquires scope no one authorized — it is recorded as a remainder with a destination instead). *Widen the tree-wide guard's allowlist to accommodate Phase 2N's new routes* (rejected, and forbidden: `OPEN_OCCURRENCES` stays empty and no allowlist may be created to accommodate a future occurrence — a new route that needs one has a defect, not an exemption).
- **Consequences.** Phase 2N moves from **planned** to **in implementation**. `phase-2n-declarations.test.ts` is **inverted rather than deleted** — the assertions that forbade acceptance records, matrices and reports become assertions that they appear at their gates, exactly as Phase 2M's equivalent was inverted at its close — while the budget assertions, the id-coherence assertions and the count are kept and strengthened. The A13 milestone-line assertion in `phase-2f-documentation.test.ts` **moves in this same commit**, citing ADR-112 and no longer requiring the word "planning", which would now *understate* the authorization; the rule it enforces is unchanged — *the line cites every authorization the phase has received and overstates none of them.* **Migrations stay at 92 and parity at `202608120092` until a slice spends an allocation.** ADR-108, ADR-109, ADR-110 and ADR-111 are **not rewritten**. **ADR-055's expiry of 2026-10-27 is unchanged** and this phase neither satisfies nor supersedes it. Signup stays closed, the rollout gate stays at 25 · 3 · 2, push stays a parallel residual, and **A13 continues to guard the roadmap successor**, which this ADR does not authorize, does not scope and does not name in its heading.

## ADR-113 — The owner authorizes M3 to create one deletion-confirmation table, and amends §6.3's "no new table" prediction without rewriting it

- **Date:** 2026-08-13
- **Status:** Accepted — an **owner decision**. It authorizes **migration M3, already allocated to slice 2N.3, to create one table** for server-issued deletion confirmations, inside M3's own file. It authorizes **nothing else**: no fourth migration, no reallocation, no change to any of the seventeen signatures, no widening of the Phase 2E task-command vocabulary, and no successor phase.
- **Context — the re-audit that produced the question.** `2N-CORRECT-013` and `OD-2N-11` **B** require the per-type consequences of deletion to be confirmed **before M3 is written**. That intermediate re-audit ran against `main` at `0f521f4` and the database M1 produced, and is recorded at `docs/reports/phase-2n/PHASE_2N_SLICE_3_DELETION_REAUDIT.md`, merged as **PR #213 at `456750b`** with CI green on that exact SHA. It found **no propagation that cannot be truthfully undone**: for each of person, project and memory the full set of destroyed and nulled rows was snapshotted, destroyed and **restored byte-identically under the same identities**, with relations, roles, validity windows and the retrieval embedding intact and **no provider call**. Its verdict was **M3 AUTORIZÁVEL DENTRO DO CONTRATO EXISTENTE**, with exactly one deviation surfaced for the owner rather than absorbed.
- **The deviation.** `PHASE_2N_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md` §6.3 predicted M3 would affect *"One new function. **No new table**, so `2N-SEC-005`'s cascade requirement is not engaged."* **That prediction is wrong.** A confirmation that is server-issued, single-use and bound to a fingerprint **is a row**, and the only such store that exists — `task_command_confirmations` — is welded to tasks by `foreign key (user_id, task_id) references public.tasks (user_id, id)` and by a **closed** `CHECK (action in ('cancel_task','create_task'))` that `taxonomy.ts:188` and `copy.ts` assert against.
- **Decision 1 — M3 creates one table, inside M3.** `public.entity_deletion_confirmations`. **It is not an additional migration and it does not change the budget:** `3 allocated · 1 spent (M1, deployed, parity 202608130093) · M3 authorized for 2N.3 · M2 reserved for 2N.7 · non-transferable · **a fourth remains a STOP CONDITION***.
- **Decision 2 — the product decision is unchanged.** `OD-2N-11` **B** already requires explicit confirmation. This ADR adds no capability; it settles the **structure** required to deliver that capability honestly: server-issued; owner-scoped; single-use; bound to the entity type and id; bound to the fingerprint of the observed state; bound to the consequences the preview showed; expiring by **factual change**, never as a portable client-held authorization; refused after consumption; refused if the payload changed; refused if it belongs to another owner; unable to authorize any other action; and persisting **no unnecessary sensitive content**.
- **Decision 3 — `task_command_confirmations` is not reused and not widened.** Phase 2E's closed vocabulary and its contracts stand untouched. Two confirmation stores with two vocabularies is the correct outcome; one table serving two unrelated authorities by convention is the defect this repository has already paid for.
- **Decision 4 — the table stores identifiers and hashes, and nothing else.** Owner; entity type; entity id; the request fingerprint; the consumption state; the idempotency key; the strictly necessary timestamps; and an unambiguous link to the operation through `(user_id, operation_key)`, which `undo_operations` **already carries**. **It stores no name, no title, no note, no memory content, no excerpt, no endpoint and no free text** — anything representable by an identifier or a hash is stored as one. The enumerated consequences are bound as a **digest**, not as stored counts, so the binding is provable and the row carries nothing to leak.
- **Decision 5 — `2N-SEC-005` is engaged and satisfied.** The table carries `user_id uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade`, so account-deletion coverage does not regress. `2N-SEC-006` is respected: **no retention value is minted and no migration schedules a sweep.**
- **Decision 6 — the amendment is traceable and the historical text is preserved.** §6.3's sentence is **kept verbatim** and annotated in place with an explicit `*(Amended by ADR-113: …)*` marker, following the mechanism ADR-109 used for twelve requirements, ADR-110 for one and ADR-112 for four. **No silent edit.** §6.5's claim that no fourth migration is hidden **still holds and is not amended** — the table fits inside M3.
- **Decision 7 — the stop condition that returns the work to the owner.** If, during implementation, the responsibilities M3 must carry — the table with its constraints, indexes, RLS and grants; preview, issuance, consumption and application; transactional deletion; the snapshots a true undo requires; the undo handlers; audit; and the cleanup of the enumerated orphaned polymorphic references — **do not fit honestly in one migration, the work stops.** Responsibilities may not be compacted artificially and a fourth migration may not be created.
- **Alternatives considered.** *Reuse `task_command_confirmations`* (rejected: FK-bound to `tasks`, and its `action` CHECK is a closed vocabulary Phase 2E's tests defend; reusing it would put deletion into the task-command taxonomy and break them). *Bind only a fingerprint the caller returns, with no row* (rejected, and it is the move `202607260059` already refused: a client-derivable value is not confirmation evidence, and single-use cannot be enforced without state). *Ship deletion without a single-use server-issued confirmation* (rejected by the owner: it contradicts the stated M3 contract and `OD-2N-11` B). *Spend a fourth migration on the table* (refused: a fourth is a stop condition, and the table belongs to the operation M3 already carries). *Edit §6.3 in place* (rejected: an implementation plan whose wrong predictions vanish teaches nothing, and this repository's mechanism for this is annotation, not overwrite).
- **Consequences.** M3 is authorized to proceed with the design the re-audit proved. Migrations stay at **93** and parity at **`202608130093`** until M3 is created, merged and deployed. `OD-2N-11` **B**, `OD-2N-14` **B** and the other fifteen signatures are **unchanged**. ADR-108 through ADR-112 are **not rewritten**. **ADR-055's expiry of 2026-10-27 is unchanged.** Signup stays closed, the rollout gate stays at **25 · 3 · 2**, push stays a parallel residual, **Phase 2O is not started, not planned and not retargeted**, and **A13 continues to guard the roadmap successor**, which this ADR does not authorize, does not scope and does not name in its heading.

## ADR-114 — The owner authorizes the Papel e Console redesign, reversing decision D5 so that dark mode ships complete rather than not at all

- **Date:** 2026-08-14
- **Status:** Accepted — an **owner decision**. It authorizes the **integral application of the approved "Papel e Console" visual direction** to the real product: design system, typography, light and dark themes, components, universal states, shell, navigation, capture, command palette, and every authenticated and unauthenticated surface the product already ships. It authorizes **no migration, no schema change, no RLS change, no new provider, no push scope, no new search index and no new data export**. It reverses **owner decision D5** and restates `2I-LANG-007`.
- **Context.** The direction arrives as a design handoff of ten documents, a token contract and five HTML mockups — documentation and reference, **not production code**. It was audited against this repository before any file was touched, and the audit's finding is the premise of this ADR: **the product already has more functional infrastructure than the handoff assumes.** Asynchronous capture, voice, the command palette, search, interpretation revisions, pending questions, selective confirmation, real undo, memory conflicts, bulk actions, planning, citations, sensitivity and the task panel by Parallel/Intercepting Routes all exist. The work is therefore **frontend and composition over preserved contracts**, not a rewrite.
- **Decision 1 — the source-of-truth order is fixed, and the mockups are last.** Real code, then database and migrations, then the permanent documentation, then the audit, then the handoff's prose, then the mockups. **Where a mockup disagrees with the handoff's documentation, the documentation wins.** The HTML is reference for look and behaviour and is **never copied into production**.
- **Decision 2 — D5 is reversed: dark mode is in scope, and it ships complete.** `2I-LANG-007` recorded that dark mode was out of scope and, correctly for that decision, `experience.css` carried **no dark tokens at all** — the guard asserted their **absence**, so that no reader could mistake a half-set for support. The redesign specifies an **authored** dark palette: warm, low-contrast at the surface, high-contrast at the text, derived by neither inversion nor filter. The requirement is therefore restated, not deleted, and **the guard inverts with it**: it now asserts that every light tone token has a dark counterpart, that the explicit and system-preference blocks stay identical, and — the control — that the dark values are **not** copies of the light ones. **The failure being prevented is unchanged. Only the direction of "partial" moved.**
- **Decision 3 — the default theme follows the machine, and an explicit choice beats it.** No stored preference means `prefers-color-scheme` decides, which costs no migration and no column. The media block is qualified `:root:not([data-theme="light"])` so that a user who chooses light on a dark machine is obeyed; without that qualifier the choice would be silently overridden, and the bug would be invisible to anyone testing on a light machine.
- **Decision 4 — the palette has exactly one home, and the nine legacy tokens become aliases over it.** `src/app/tokens.css` is the only file that may hold a raw colour. `--ink`, `--ink-soft`, `--line`, `--paper`, `--mist`, `--white`, `--blue`, `--blue-wash` and `--signal` — **616 uses across 22 stylesheets** — are redefined in terms of it and hold no values of their own. This is what makes the redesign landable in reviewable slices: an untouched surface renders correctly in **both** themes, and each slice retires the aliases it replaces. `--white` is deliberately **not** `#fff`; it names a surface, and a surface in dark mode is dark.
- **Decision 5 — the type families change and Newsreader stays.** Manrope is replaced by IBM Plex Sans and JetBrains Mono by IBM Plex Mono, both via `next/font` with enumerated weights, because neither Plex family is variable on Google Fonts and an unenumerated weight is a build error while an unused one is dead payload. **Newsreader is kept** — it already carries the user's own content and is what is recognisable in the product. Stylesheets now read the **semantic** aliases `--font-reading`, `--font-ui` and `--font-mono` rather than family names, so a future family change touches one file rather than eighteen.
- **Decision 6 — the thirteen open handoff questions are settled by standing defaults rather than by stopping.** Brain is built as a unified experience with **every current URL preserved**; Conversar keeps `/app/chat` and its conversation route; Empresas stays a low-priority lens with its detail pages intact; Contextos is presented as a filter while its routes and deep links survive; density is **comfortable** by default and compact only where genuinely supported; voice keeps its exact functional contract, retention, storage and provider; search and the palette are reused with **no new index**; push gains no functional scope; **no new export is implemented and no false control is shown**; icons stay **lucide-react**, already installed; and the existing brand is adapted provisionally rather than replaced.
- **Decision 7 — what is excluded, and stays excluded.** Offline capture, offline-first, any local queue, migrations, schema changes, RLS changes, a new provider, new push, a new data export, a different audio retention, new search indexes — and, above all, **any control that would reproduce a mockup by pretending a capability exists**. Where a mockup shows something unbuilt, the honest state is designed instead; **a clickable affordance that does nothing is forbidden.**
- **Decision 8 — every inviolable contract is preserved.** Authentication, RLS, per-route/Action/RPC/worker authorization, existing Server Actions and RPCs, idempotency, audit, real undo, asynchronous processing, the immutable original, append-only revisions, human confirmation, provenance, citations, sensitivity, `ProtectedContent`, BYOK, profile timezone, local-day semantics, pagination, quotas, retry and failure states, consent, account lifecycle, CAPTCHA, CSP and bilingual behaviour. **Authorization does not move to the client, no parallel write path is created, and a real undo is never replaced by a cosmetic toast.**
- **Decision 9 — the stop conditions that return the work to the owner.** A migration that was not foreseen; any change to RLS, authentication, consent, providers, secrets, the service worker, push or an external contract; a concrete risk of losing functionality or data; a product decision the standing defaults do not resolve; or a test revealing an architectural conflict that would require widening scope.
- **Alternatives considered.** *Keep D5 and ship light only* (rejected: the direction specifies dark as a first-class authored palette, and the owner has now asked for it explicitly — the guard's job is to prevent a **partial** dark mode, which it still does). *Weaken the 2I guard to let dark through* (rejected outright, and it is the trap this repository has paid for before: a guard that is loosened to accommodate new work stops being evidence. It was **inverted** instead, with a two-sided control that fails both on a missing dark token and on a dark block that merely repeats the light values). *Add a second palette beside the existing tone tokens* (rejected: two sources of state colour is precisely the drift Phase 2I existed to remove; the tone tokens were **re-tinted in place** and `experience.css` remains the single source). *Rewrite the 22 stylesheets surface by surface before shipping anything* (rejected: one unreviewable megadiff, and every intermediate state broken — the alias bridge delivers the same end state in slices that each stay green). *Copy the mockups' HTML* (rejected: it encodes capabilities the product does not have, and it is not production code).
- **Consequences.** `src/app/tokens.css` is created and imported first. `experience.css` is re-tinted and gains a complete dark set. `phase-2i-experience-guard.test.ts`'s `2I-LANG-007` block is **inverted, not deleted**, and gains a control. A new `stylesheet-registry-guard.test.ts` closes a real gap the redesign exposed: four Playwright lanes inline the product's CSS from hand-written arrays that nothing connected to the imports, so moving the palette would have left every lane measuring **unstyled markup and passing for the wrong reason**. **Migrations stay at 94 and parity at `202608140094`** — this initiative spends none. **Phase 2N stays closed** and this ADR neither reopens it nor claims its remainders. ADR-108 through ADR-113 are **not rewritten**. **ADR-055's expiry of 2026-10-27 is unchanged.** Signup stays closed, the rollout gate stays at **25 · 3 · 2**, push stays a parallel residual, and **A13 continues to guard the roadmap successor**, which this ADR does not authorize, does not scope and does not name in its heading.

## ADR-115 — The owner authorizes Phase 2O — Activation, preferences, and control as a planning phase, and the phase-start guard retargets to the roadmap successor

- **Date:** 2026-08-15
- **Status:** Accepted — an **owner decision**. It authorizes **planning only**: an audit of the live product, a PRD, an implementation plan, a threat model, a traceability contract, the governance guards that enforce them, and the retarget of **A13**. It authorizes **no implementation, no product-code change, no migration, no deploy, no secret change, and no opening of signup.** The twelve owner decisions this package raises are **OPEN and unsigned**; implementation needs its own authorization.
- **Context — the baseline moved, and it moved after the predecessor closed.** Phase 2N closed on 2026-08-14 at `9b7cda7`. On 2026-08-15 the **Papel e Console redesign** (ADR-114) merged at `9cc1175` — 39 commits, 138 files, zero migrations. Two things Etapa 6 was going to build now partly exist: a **transparency centre**, shipped as Dados e IA over `/app/history`, `/app/costs` and `/app/jobs` with every URL preserved, and **Ajustes as a real destination**. The re-audit was therefore run against the tree rather than against the roadmap, and the roadmap's Etapa 6 is **corrected in six places** by `docs/reports/phase-2o/PHASE_2O_CURRENT_EXPERIENCE_AUDIT.md` §8.
- **Decision 1 — the scope is Etapa 6 as corrected by the audit, and the tree outranks the roadmap.** Where a roadmap item is already built, it is recorded as `baseline` and not rebuilt. Where a roadmap item contradicts a signed outcome — `planning_day` and `planning_time`, retired by `2M-AUDIT-005` — it is **not built by rule**.
- **Decision 2 — the phase is planning-only, and the package proves it.** 113 requirements across sixteen families and nine slices are **declared and none authorized**. No product code changes in the authorizing commit; the only source files it touches are governance guards.
- **Decision 3 — A13 retargets in this commit, to the roadmap successor.** An accepted ADR naming the authorized phase is signal 3, and its PRD and plan are signals 1 and 2, so the guard must move in the same change that records the authorization and the invariant is never unenforced in between. This is the **tenth** application of that rule. **The roadmap ends at Phase 2O**, so the successor the guard now protects is not a scoped phase — it is the next name the lettered series would take, protected **in advance and precisely because nothing has scoped it**. This ADR does not authorize it, does not scope it, and **does not name it in this heading**.
- **Decision 4 — the twelve decisions are the owner's, and the agent signs none.** `OD-2O-1` … `OD-2O-12` each carry options, a recommendation and a consequence. A recommendation is not an outcome. No requirement is marked authorized before an answer, and the declined options stay visible so the decision remains reviewable.
- **Decision 5 — the proposed budget is two, and it is unsigned.** **2 allocated · obligation ZERO · NON-TRANSFERABLE**, and **zero created by this package**. **M1** for an activation telemetry vocabulary, conditional on a real producer *and* a real consumer. **M2** reserved for **exactly one** of a persisted appearance preference, stored onboarding progress, or an export job — whichever single option the owner signs. **A third is a STOP CONDITION.** An allocation may close unspent, and closing unspent is a correct outcome; an unnecessary spend is a defect.
- **Decision 6 — signup does not open, by this ADR or by inference.** The rollout gate stands at **25 pass · 3 fail · 2 owner-signature** and is untouched. `RG-DEP-1`, `RG-DEP-3`, `RG-QUO-3`, `RG-LEG-4` and `RG-DEP-4` remain owner and operator work and **may not be closed by writing a file**. A retention sweep schedule, if ever armed, is armed by an operator script and never by a migration, because scheduling is authorization.
- **Decision 7 — the inherited residuals are not absorbed.** `2N-RELATION-TRIGGER`, `2N-IDENTITY-EXTRACTION`, `2N-FILES-WRITER`, `2N-MOBILE`, `2N-PRIVACY-FREETEXT`, `2N-RELATION-END-ANNOUNCEMENT`, the push HTTP 403 on a real iPhone with Android never executed, the unexecuted screen-reader session, `T-19`'s unscheduled retention sweeps, and **ADR-055 expiring 2026-10-27 neither satisfied nor superseded** all stay open and stay named. `OD-2O-11` offers each **explicitly, with its cost**, and backlog does not become scope by proximity.
- **Decision 8 — one divergence between a signed decision and the tree is recorded rather than resolved here.** ADR-114 Decision 3 fixed that the theme follows the machine **and an explicit choice beats it**. The CSS for both halves ships, including the `:root:not([data-theme="light"])` qualifier. **Nothing in the product writes `data-theme`** — a census over 920 files returns none — so the explicit choice has no control. This ADR does not build it; it records the gap and routes it to `OD-2O-2`.
- **Alternatives considered.** *Authorize implementation now* (rejected: twelve decisions are open, three of them change the migration budget, and one determines whether the export is synchronous or a job — authorizing implementation over open decisions is how a budget gets spent on a shape nobody chose). *Reuse the Phase 2N audit and skip the re-audit* (rejected: 138 files changed after that audit, and two of this phase's subjects moved). *Keep A13 on Phase 2O and add an exemption for the authorized artifacts* (rejected outright — an exemption is the too-weak half ADR-067 removed, and the resolution is authorization, never a looser pattern). *Retarget A13 to a range covering every remaining letter* (rejected: `phase-2x` is a real historical directory in this repository and a range would collide with it; one letter, moved once per authorization, is the shape nine prior applications have proved). *Fold the Phase 2N remainders into this phase* (rejected: each needs a migration or new authority, and none is transferable into either allocation — absorbing them silently would spend a budget the owner has not signed on work the owner has not scoped).
- **Consequences.** `docs/initiatives/phase-2o/PHASE_2O_PRD.md` and `..._IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md` become the governing pair; the audit, gaps, threat model and traceability contract are filed under `docs/reports/phase-2o/`. `src/lib/closeout/phase-2o-declarations.test.ts` enforces the planning-only posture in both directions, including the **absence** of any closing artifact. **A13 moves off Phase 2O in this same commit.** **Migrations stay at 94 and hosted parity at `202608140094`**; this package creates none. ADR-108 through ADR-114 are **not rewritten**. Phase 2N stays **COMPLETE** and this ADR neither reopens it nor claims its remainders. Signup stays closed, the rollout gate stays **25 · 3 · 2**, push stays a parallel residual, ADR-055's expiry of **2026-10-27** is unchanged, and **the roadmap successor stays unauthorized, unscoped and unnamed by this heading**.
- **Superseded in part by ADR-116** (2026-08-15), which signs all twelve decisions this ADR left open. **ADR-115 is not rewritten**: its ceiling of two, its refusals and its planning-only posture are the record of what was true when it was signed, and ADR-116 states what changed rather than editing this text into agreement with itself.

## ADR-116 — The owner signs all twelve Phase 2O decisions, and the second allocation loses its only destinations

- **Date:** 2026-08-15
- **Status:** Accepted — an **owner decision**. It signs `OD-2O-1` … `OD-2O-12` in full and **authorizes no implementation**: Phase 2O remains a **planning phase** under ADR-115, no migration may be created, no deploy may run, signup does not open, and the roadmap successor is not started. **ADR-115 is amended by addition, not rewritten.**
- **Context.** ADR-115 authorized planning with every decision open — the first phase in this repository authorized in that state. The package's own guard forbade any document from reading a recommendation as an outcome. This ADR is the owner's answer, and it takes every recommendation.
- **Decision 1 — the twelve signatures.** `OD-2O-1` **A** (a static public entry page in both locales, **no signup call to action while signup is closed**) · `OD-2O-2` **A** (an appearance choice held client-side in `localStorage`, applied before first paint, **no column and no migration**) · `OD-2O-3` **A** (onboarding progress **derived** from existing data) · `OD-2O-4` **A** (export **synchronous and server-side**, over the deletion enumeration) · `OD-2O-5` **A** (say the user is signed in and offer a **global** sign-out; **no administrative device list**) · `OD-2O-6` **A** (controls **only** for the three review preferences that already have consumers) · `OD-2O-7` **A** (keep the nine consumer-less columns, offer no control, **guard the absence**) · `OD-2O-8` **A** (an activation funnel **only if** a real producer and a real consumer both ship) · `OD-2O-9` (**2 allocated · obligation ZERO · NON-TRANSFERABLE**, a third a **STOP CONDITION**) · `OD-2O-10` **A** (the readiness dossier only) · `OD-2O-11` (admit **two**, decline the rest) · `OD-2O-12` **B** (the screen-reader session is executed **or** explicitly recorded as not executed, and **does not alone block closeout**).
- **Decision 2 — `OD-2O-11` admits exactly two, and the "only" is load-bearing.** Admitted: **the 21px mobile touch target** (`2N-MOBILE`, `online-memories.spec.ts:85`) and **a real screen-reader validation**. Declined by name: `2N-RELATION-TRIGGER`, `2N-IDENTITY-EXTRACTION`, `2N-FILES-WRITER`, retention sweeps and their scheduling, and any resolution of **ADR-055**, which stays recorded and unresolved. **Declined by the word "only", and named here so the exclusion is recorded rather than inferred:** `2N-PRIVACY-FREETEXT`, `2N-RELATION-END-ANNOUNCEMENT`, and the push HTTP 403 / Android-never-executed track.
- **Decision 3 — the screen-reader gate may never be promoted by anything but a run.** Under `OD-2O-12` **B** the session does not on its own block closeout, and the price of that is an absolute prohibition: `2O-ACCESS-006` may close `built` **only** on a recorded execution naming device, software and version. It may **never** be promoted to a pass by documentation, by an emulator, by an automated accessibility scan, or by inference from one. Absent a run, it closes **`partial`** with the remainder stated and a destination — never `built`, and never quietly absent.
- **Decision 4 — the second allocation has no remaining destination, and that is the expected close.** ADR-115 reserved **M2** for exactly one of `OD-2O-2` **B**, `OD-2O-3` **B** or `OD-2O-4` **B**. **All three are signed A.** The budget stays at two because the owner signed two and it is non-transferable — so **M2 now has no destination it may be spent on, and closing it unspent is the correct outcome rather than an omission.** Spending it on anything else is a **STOP CONDITION**. `M1` remains live and conditional on `OD-2O-8` **A** delivering a real producer *and* a real consumer; if it does not, **both allocations close unspent**, and that too is correct.
- **Decision 5 — three requirements are added, and nothing is renumbered.** `OD-2O-2` **A** signed a capability the PRD carried no requirement for, because it was conditional. `2O-PREF-013`, `-014` and `-015` are **appended** to an existing family. No identifier is renumbered, reused or deleted. The total moves **113 → 116** and `2O-PREF` moves **12 → 15**.
- **Decision 6 — one interpretation is stated rather than assumed, and the owner may reverse it cheaply.** `OD-2O-6` was framed over **inert** preferences; its signature reads *"controls only for the three review preferences that already have real consumers."* `embedding_model` is **not** inert — it has six behavioural consumers and no control — and `2O-AICONFIG-004` offered a disjunction: a control, or a registry row recording why not. **The conservative reading is taken:** the word "only" reaches `embedding_model` too, so it gains a **registry row and no control**, and `2O-AICONFIG-004`'s disjunction is resolved in place. This is an **interpretation, not a signature**, it is the reading that adds no scope, and reversing it costs one form field.
- **Decision 7 — what the signatures do not create.** No migration. No new authority for `authenticated`. No new `SECURITY DEFINER` function: `OD-2O-4` **A** reuses the deletion **enumeration**, not necessarily its definer, and if the export cannot be made complete and tenant-safe without new authority, that is a **STOP CONDITION** rather than a quiet grant. No CSP change: the anti-flash script `OD-2O-2` **A** needs is possible because `next.config.ts` already carries `'unsafe-inline'` in `script-src` — **verified in the tree rather than assumed** — and `csp.test.ts` continues to hold the header shape unchanged.
- **Decision 8 — one consequence of `OD-2O-2` **A** is stated plainly rather than discovered later.** A client-held appearance choice **does not follow the account across devices**. That is the cost of spending no migration, the owner accepted it, and no document may describe the preference as if it were an account setting.
- **Alternatives considered.** *Reduce the budget to one, since M2 has no destination* (rejected: the owner signed two, an allocation may close unspent by design, and silently shrinking a signed budget would be the agent re-deciding). *Resolve `OD-2O-6`'s reach over `embedding_model` by giving it a control* (rejected: it widens scope on an interpretation rather than a signature, and the conservative reading is reversible while a shipped control is not). *Fold the declined residuals in anyway, since the phase touches their surfaces* (rejected: `OD-2O-11`'s "only" is the whole point, and proximity is not authorization). *Rewrite ADR-115's budget clause to say M2 is dead* (rejected: an accepted ADR is not edited into agreement with a later one; this ADR states the change and ADR-115 keeps its record).
- **Consequences.** The PRD, implementation plan, audit, threat model and traceability contract are updated in the same commit as this ADR, and the phase's guard **inverts** its unsigned-decision refusal into a signed-decision refusal rather than deleting it. **116 requirements across sixteen families and nine slices.** Estimate **14–19.5 weeks** — and the previously published 13–18 was an arithmetic error against the plan's own table, corrected here rather than carried. **Migrations stay at 94, hosted parity at `202608140094`, and none is created.** ADR-108 through ADR-115 are **not rewritten**. Signup stays **closed**, the rollout gate stays **25 · 3 · 2**, ADR-055 stays neither satisfied nor superseded and expires **2026-10-27**, **implementation is still not authorized**, and **the roadmap successor stays unauthorized, unscoped and unnamed by this heading**.
- **Amended by ADR-117** (2026-08-15), which converts Decision 6's flagged interpretation into an owner confirmation and adds one prohibition. **ADR-116 is not rewritten**: Decision 6 is the honest record of what was an agent reading at the moment it was written.

## ADR-117 — The owner confirms the `embedding_model` reading, and forbids touching the column to satisfy it

- **Date:** 2026-08-15
- **Status:** Accepted — an **owner decision**, taken on the one interpretation ADR-116 deliberately left flagged. It settles `2O-AICONFIG-004` and **authorizes no implementation, no migration, no column change, no schema change and no new authority**. It alters none of the twelve signatures and adds no requirement.
- **Context.** ADR-116 Decision 6 recorded that `OD-2O-6` was framed over **inert** preferences, that `embedding_model` is **not** inert — six behavioural consumers and no control — and that the conservative reading was taken **by the agent, not the owner**. The package said so in the ADR, the PRD and the audit rather than resolving it silently, precisely so this confirmation could be asked for. This ADR is that confirmation, and it goes one clause further than the question asked.
- **Decision 1 — the reading is confirmed and is now the owner's.** `embedding_model` **gains no control in Phase 2O**. The word *only* in `OD-2O-6` reaches it.
- **Decision 2 — it appears in the capability registry, and the row says the true thing.** The row records that `embedding_model` **has real consumers and no authorized control** — not that it has no consumer, which would be false, and not that it is inert, which it is not. This is the distinction `2O-ACTIVATION-005`'s tree-derived `consumerEvidence` exists to keep honest: a row claiming no consumer for a column with six would fail the guard.
- **Decision 3 — only the three review preferences gain controls.** `daily_review_time`, `weekly_review_time` and `weekly_review_day`, and nothing else, in this phase.
- **Decision 4 — and this authorizes nothing about the column itself.** `embedding_model` **may not be removed, altered, renamed, defaulted differently, or migrated** to satisfy this decision. The absence of a control is not a licence to tidy the schema, and a migration proposed to "clean up" an uncontrolled column would be spending an allocation the owner did not authorize on work nobody asked for.
- **Alternatives considered.** *Give it a control anyway, since it has consumers* (rejected by the owner: it widens the phase on a reading rather than a signature, and `OD-2O-6`'s "only" was deliberate). *Leave the interpretation flagged and merge as it stands* (rejected: the PRD would carry, in `main`, a statement that the reading is the agent's when the owner has confirmed it — and a guard would hold that false statement in place). *Drop the column, since nothing controls it* (rejected explicitly by Decision 4: it has six real consumers, and removing it is neither authorized nor wanted).
- **Consequences.** `2O-AICONFIG-004` is **restated in place** with an `ADR-117` marker and its superseded text retained; **no identifier is renumbered, reused or deleted, and the total stays 116.** The phase guard's assertion inverts from *"this is flagged as an interpretation"* to *"this is confirmed as an owner decision, and the prohibition is recorded"* — inverted rather than deleted, for the reason every inversion in this phase has been kept. ADR-115 and ADR-116 are **not rewritten**. **Migrations stay at 94, parity `202608140094`, none created.** Signup stays **closed**, the rollout gate stays **25 · 3 · 2**, **implementation is still not authorized**, and the roadmap successor stays unauthorized, unscoped and unnamed by this heading.
- **Superseded in part by ADR-118** (2026-08-15), which authorizes implementation. **ADR-117 is not rewritten**: its refusal of implementation is the record of what was true when it was signed, and its four decisions about `embedding_model` are unchanged and binding for the whole phase.

## ADR-118 — The owner authorizes Phase 2O implementation through closeout, and the planning-only refusals invert rather than being deleted

- **Date:** 2026-08-15
- **Status:** Accepted — an **owner decision**. It authorizes **implementation through closeout** of Phase 2O: product code, pages, components, Server Actions, copy modules, tests, browser journeys, guards, branches, pull requests, merges, CI on the exact merge SHAs, per-slice acceptance records, the traceability matrix, the readiness dossier, the closing report and — only if slice 2O.8's conditions are met — one migration, its deployment and a deployment record. It authorizes **nothing else**: no third migration, no spend of **M2**, no successor phase, no opening of signup, no change to the rollout gate, no CSP change, no change to `embedding_model`, no resumption of the push investigation, and no absorption of a declined residual.
- **Context — the plan's own preconditions, checked rather than asserted.** `PHASE_2O_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md` §8 lists six. Two were already discharged by ADR-116 and `OD-2O-9`. The remaining four were verified at this baseline before any file changed: **`main` = `origin/main` = `57beb06`**, worktree clean, **no open PR**; **CI green on both merge SHAs** — `e4f2668`, on all three job families, and `57beb06`; **94 local migrations = 94 hosted, parity `202608140094`**, read live from the project rather than from a document; and the **rollout gate re-read by running `npm run rollout:verify`**, standing at **25 pass · 3 fail · 2 owner-signature**, with `RG-QUO-3`, `RG-DEP-1` and `RG-DEP-3` failing and `RG-LEG-4` and `RG-DEP-4` unsigned. The sixth precondition is this ADR.
- **The re-audit found the tree unmoved where it matters.** The delta from the plan's baseline `9cc1175` to `57beb06` is **documentation and two governance guards**; not one line of product code changed. The audit that governs this phase therefore still describes the tree it was written against, and no finding had to be re-executed.
- **Decision 1 — implementation is authorized slice by slice, in the planned order.** `2O.0` foundations, then `2O.1` entry, `2O.2` the first conquest, `2O.3` the preferences centre, `2O.4` AI and cost, `2O.5` privacy and data, `2O.6` notifications and recovery, `2O.7` mobile and accessibility, `2O.8` readiness and closeout. Each slice is re-audited against the `main` the previous one produced. The order may change **only where a re-audit proves a real dependency**, with the reason recorded, and **a reordering may not be used to widen scope**.
- **Decision 2 — the twelve signatures and ADR-117's confirmation stand unchanged.** None is reopened, narrowed, widened or reinterpreted. `OD-2O-1` … `OD-2O-12` are re-audited against this `main` and every one of them still describes the tree it was signed over.
- **Decision 3 — the migration budget is unchanged: `2 allocated · obligation ZERO · 0 spent · NON-TRANSFERABLE`.** **M1** is live and conditional on slice 2O.8 delivering a **real producer and a real consumer**; absent either, `2O-METRICS-001` … `-005` close `not-built-by-rule` and M1 closes unspent. **M2 has no destination and may not be spent on anything** (`R-2O-25`); it closes unspent by construction, and that is the expected close rather than an omission. **A third migration is a STOP CONDITION.**
