- **Decision 2 — the cause, established by elimination rather than asserted.** `e2e/accessibility.spec.ts` strips `@import "tailwindcss"` from the CSS it inlines, and **Tailwind's preflight is what makes a form control inherit its colour** (`button,input,optgroup,select,textarea{font:inherit;color:inherit}`). Without it a `<select>` falls back to the UA's `FieldText`: white under Chromium's dark `color-scheme`, **black** under WebKit's — 1.13:1 on the dark canvas, on exactly the two surfaces whose text sits on a select. A second, independent lane artifact was found alongside it: a `setContent` document carrying a viewport meta with no real origin makes WebKit resolve **no custom properties at all** on `body` and on form controls, which is why the first measurements reported `#ffffff` backgrounds that do not exist.
- **Decision 3 — no product colour, token, CSS or component may be changed to satisfy a fixture.** Changing a token to make a wrong test pass damages the product to protect a lane, which is the inversion this repository exists to prevent. `src/lib/closeout/phase-2q-accessibility-lane.test.ts` pins the two surfaces' rules and the dark palette's tokens **byte-for-byte**, so the prohibition is checkable rather than promised.
- **Decision 4 — the lane is corrected to apply the theme by the product's own mechanism.** It stamped `data-theme` into an HTML string; the product stores a choice in `localStorage` and a pre-paint script reads it. The lane now seeds the choice and runs **`APPEARANCE_SCRIPT` imported from `src/features/appearance/contracts.ts`** — the product's code, not a copy, so the two cannot drift. It also loads a real document on the app origin before replacing it, which is both what a real navigation does and what makes `localStorage` reachable.
- **Decision 5 — axe is not weakened in any respect.** No rule is disabled beyond the pre-existing `region`, no threshold is relaxed below `serious`, no selector is excluded, no surface leaves the scanned list, and no `skip` is added. Control 3 plants a **real** contrast failure and requires axe to still report it, with a passing pair as the two-sided half.
- **Decision 6 — CI widens only after the lane is faithful and green, and only for that lane.** `ci.yml` installs `webkit` and runs `accessibility.spec.ts` on the `iphone-emulated` project. The other journeys stay on the two Chromium projects. **The order is the requirement**, as ADR-127 Decision 6 said: widening first would have landed a red pipeline on a defect that was not real.
- **Decision 7 — the requirement classifications follow the measurement, not the plan.** `2Q-ACCESS-001` is **built** as an executed investigation: the false positive was reproduced, located and compared against the real application. `2Q-ACCESS-002` and `2Q-ACCESS-003` are **baseline** — global search and the Work bulk bar already had correct contrast on real WebKit and **no product fix was needed**; neither may be classified `built`, because that would claim a change that did not happen. `2Q-ACCESS-004` is **built** — the lane fix plus the CI widening. `2Q-ACCESS-005` records honestly that **none of this is screen-reader evidence**.
- **Decision 8 — the waiver does not move.** `2P-ACCESS-005` stays **WAIVED, NOT PASSED**. A lane running on a third engine is exactly the kind of change a later reader might mistake for screen-reader coverage, so the disclaimer is asserted in the suite rather than left to prose.
- **Decision 9 — every intermediate measurement is preserved as history.** The investigation reached three different intermediate conclusions before the final one, two of them wrong, each with a plausible artifact behind it. They are recorded in the slice's acceptance record rather than tidied away: a report that showed only the final answer would teach nothing about how a lane can invent a defect, and the corrections are the most useful part of the finding.
- **Decision 10 — nothing else moves.** No migration; the budget stays **1 allocated · 1 spent** and a second remains a stop condition. No BYOK credit and no paid AI call. Signup stays closed, the rollout gate stays 25 pass · 3 fail · 2 owner-signature, `RG-DEP-3` still cannot be closed by writing a file, push HTTP 403 is not resumed, `2P-REVIEW-CITATIONS` stays **NOT DELIVERED** until the owner sees it on their own device, and **no successor phase is started or planned**.
- **Alternatives considered.** Add `color: var(--text-primary)` to the two select rules (rejected: the product already resolves correctly, so the declaration would be a change made to satisfy a lane, and the fixture would still measure something the product does not do). Give the fixture an explicit body background (rejected: measured, it does not fix it — the failure is inheritance into form controls, not the canvas). Convert the dark scan to real routes as `phase-2o-mobile-accessibility.spec.ts` does (rejected **for this slice only**: it is the more robust shape and is substantially larger than the one this authorization covers; it stays available as a later initiative). Route `A11Y-WEBKIT-DARK-CONTRAST` out of the phase entirely (rejected: the lane's blind spot is real and worth closing, and closing it is what `2Q-ACCESS-004` asks for).
- **Consequences.** The accessibility lane becomes faithful to the product on the one axis where it was not, and CI gains the engine family Safari renders with — so the class of defect that hid here for a phase cannot hide again. `2Q-ACCESS-002` and `-003` close as **baseline**, which is the honest class for a surface that was already correct. Phase 2Q reaches **37 of 42** requirements classified with slice 2Q.5 remaining. **Parity stays `202608210100`, 100 local = 100 hosted.**

## ADR-130 — The owner closes Phase 2Q after validating all eight checkpoint items on their own device, and `2P-REVIEW-CITATIONS` is delivered

- **Date:** 2026-08-21
- **Status:** Accepted — an **owner decision**. It closes Phase 2Q, records the device validation that closed it, and marks `2P-REVIEW-CITATIONS` **DELIVERED**. **It authorizes no successor phase**, no further migration, no rollout change and no signup change. ADR-126, ADR-127, ADR-128 and ADR-129 are **not edited**: the rule since ADR-108 holds, and each stands as signed.
- **Context.** ADR-128 conditioned the closeout on a final owner checkpoint, and slice 2Q.5 delivered an eight-item script for the owner's own device rather than declaring the phase complete. The owner has run it. **All eight items are approved.**

- **Decision 1 — the eight items are recorded explicitly, as approved.** A new review generated **with its sources**; the **task link opened the correct task**; the **record link opened the correct record**; a **historical review showed the correct message**; a **removed source degraded with no broken link**; **no preview, title, excerpt or reveal button appeared**; the **mobile layout** is approved; and **contrast in Safari in dark mode** is approved. Recorded item by item because a phase that reported "checkpoint passed" without saying what passed would be describing a validation nobody can audit.
- **Decision 2 — `2P-REVIEW-CITATIONS` is DELIVERED, by real owner validation.** ADR-125 Decision 4 set the bar: delivered when a review **on the owner's own device** offers a working link to a real record, and a "Fontes" section without canonical links in it is not delivery. Items 1, 2 and 3 are that bar, met. **It is not delivered by an agent run, by a green pipeline or by this ADR** — it is delivered by the owner having clicked the link.
- **Decision 3 — the agent made no AI call, and that stands unchanged by the closure.** The single call in this phase was the **owner's**, at checkpoint item 1. The artifact it left on the deployed database was afterwards read **for shape only** — envelope version, declared reach, evidence state, cited type and the reference key set — with **no title, no content and no identifier of any cited record** read. Reading the shape of what the owner's call produced is a different act from making one, and it is recorded as such rather than blurred into "the proof was obtained".
- **Decision 4 — that shape is the real-boundary proof three requirements could not otherwise have.** The owner's review declares reach `["entry","task"]` — `REVIEW_REACH`, **not chat's** — evidence `evidenced`, cites a **`task`** (`2Q-CITE-007` at the real producer, the exact defect the phase existed to prevent), and carries **exactly four identifier keys with no content-bearing field** (`2Q-CITE-006` at the real producer). The pre-phase review still carries `[]` (`2Q-CITE-008`, observed in production).
- **Decision 5 — VoiceOver is still WAIVED, NOT PASSED.** Item 8 approved **contrast in Safari**, which is not a screen-reader test. `2P-ACCESS-005` does not move, and **nothing in Phase 2Q — including an accessibility lane that now runs on WebKit in CI — may ever be reported as screen-reader evidence.** A lane on a third engine is exactly the change a later reader might mistake for coverage, which is why the suite asserts the disclaimer rather than leaving it to prose.
- **Decision 6 — no remainder is silently absorbed, and this closure discharges none of them.** `2P-ATTENTION-008`'s back-navigation half stays open and **narrower than Phase 2P recorded** — slice 2Q.0 proved refresh *is* covered in a browser and back navigation is covered nowhere. `RG-DEP-3` stays a rollout matter and **cannot be closed by writing a file**; this report does not. Push HTTP 403 stays external; `2P-CHAT-007-JOURNEY` stays unspendable; `2P-AUTONOMY-005/-006/-007/-008` stay `not-built-by-rule` under ADR-123 Decision 3; `2P-REMINDER-RECURRENCE` and `2P-CALENDAR-MONTH-TELEMETRY` stay refused by the decisions that refused them; the four automation review flows stay out under `OD-2Q-8`; and converting the accessibility dark scan to **real routes** stays a later initiative at the owner's discretion.
- **Decision 7 — the budget is spent and closed.** **1 allocated · 1 spent · 1 applied**: `202608210100_phase_2q_slice_1_summary_citations.sql`. Parity `202608210100`, **100 local = 100 hosted**, read live at closeout. **No further migration is authorized by this ADR**, and Phase 2Q creates none after this.
- **Decision 8 — nothing else moves.** Signup stays closed. The rollout gate stays **25 pass · 3 fail · 2 owner-signature**. All six automation categories stay `suggest_only` with no automatic writer. No grant, RLS policy, retention rule or authority moves. No audio is persisted. Hosted residue is **zero**, proved by a two-sided control that planted four markers, removed them, and confirmed the probes could still see.
- **Decision 9 — the successor phase is NOT started, NOT planned and NOT named as active.** A read-only re-audit records the state rather than changing it: no successor directory under `docs/initiatives/` or `docs/reports/`, no successor requirement namespace in any governing artifact, and the successor's letter appears **only inside the A13 detector** — which is where it belongs, since a detector that could not name its target could not detect anything. The phase-start guard stays exactly where ADR-126 Decision 5 put it. **This ADR names no successor in its heading or its body**, for the reason ADR-126 gave: an authorizing ADR that named the phase it was handing the guard to would start that phase in the act of authorizing this one.
- **Alternatives considered.** Close the phase when CI went green on the last merge SHA (rejected: ADR-128 Decision 8 conditioned the closeout on the owner, and every technical gate being green is precisely the state in which a wrong claim is easiest to make). Mark `2P-REVIEW-CITATIONS` delivered when the browser journey clicked a link and landed (rejected: ADR-125 Decision 4 says *the owner's own device*, and a Playwright run on a synthetic fixture is not that). Absorb `2P-ATTENTION-008`'s remainder now that it is better understood (rejected: understanding a remainder more precisely is not discharging it, and this phase was told not to become a container for the project's debt).
- **Consequences.** Phase 2Q is **CLOSED**: 42 declared, 42 classified, zero unclassified, zero `partial`, zero `undelivered`; one migration allocated, spent and applied; CI green 3/3 on every one of the eight merge SHAs; hosted residue zero. **`2P-REVIEW-CITATIONS` moves from NOT DELIVERED to DELIVERED after two phases**, on the owner's own device. `2P-ACCESS-005` remains **WAIVED, NOT PASSED**. **A start signal for the roadmap successor appearing in this repository remains a defect** until the owner authorizes one.

## ADR-131 — The owner authorizes Phase 2R planning, corrects the automation record three ADRs got wrong, and retargets the phase-start guard to the roadmap successor

- **Date:** 2026-08-22
- **Status:** Accepted — an **owner decision**. It authorizes **planning only**: a PRD, an implementation plan, an audit, a gaps report, a threat model, a traceability contract, a theme comparison and the documentary guards that keep them fail-closed. **It authorizes no implementation, no migration, no deployment, no hosted data change, no signup change, no rollout change, no resumption of the push HTTP 403 investigation, no restore drill, no BYOK spend, and no successor phase.**
- **Context.** ADR-130 closed Phase 2Q and its Decision 9 recorded that the successor was *"NOT started, NOT planned and NOT named as active"*. The owner has now authorized planning for it. **ADR-126, ADR-127, ADR-128, ADR-129 and ADR-130 are not edited** — the rule since ADR-108 is that an accepted ADR in this series is never rewritten into agreement with a later one. Each stands as signed, and the one factual correction this ADR carries is made **by appending**, exactly as ADR-129 corrected ADR-127's premise.

- **Decision 1 — Phase 2R is authorized for PLANNING.** Its subject is derived from a measured census of the product, not from a roadmap: `PHASE_2K_2O_ROADMAP_DESIGN.md` **ends at Phase 2O** and names nothing beyond it, so there is no roadmap definition to inherit. The governing pair is `docs/initiatives/phase-2r/PHASE_2R_PRD.md` and `PHASE_2R_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md`; the evidence lives in `docs/reports/phase-2r/`. **73 requirements across ten families and six slices, none implemented and none classified.**

- **Decision 2 — the theme is OPEN, and is `OD-2R-1`.** The package is written for **Rotina — what repeats**, the recommendation, and the recommendation **is not a signature**. Three alternatives are costed in `PHASE_2R_THEME_OPTIONS.md`. Choosing B, C or D **replaces** the requirement set rather than amending it. The recommendation rests on one measurement rather than on preference: the deployed database holds **zero** calibration observations, **2** reminders and **2** summaries, so any theme whose completion depends on accumulated usage is not buildable now — which disqualifies the otherwise most valuable option.

- **Decision 3 — the migration budget is PROPOSED, not signed.** The package proposes **exactly one**, whose sole destination is the recurrence model. Necessity is proved rather than asserted: `public.reminders` holds twelve columns, none of which can express repetition, there is no `jsonb` column on the table to repurpose, and no `rrule`, `recurrence` or `repeat_*` exists anywhere in `supabase/migrations/`. **This ADR allocates none.** The allocation is `OD-2R-7` and requires the owner's signature in a separate implementation decision. **A second migration of any kind is a stop condition.**

- **Decision 4 — nine decisions are OPEN.** `OD-2R-1` … `OD-2R-9` are declared in PRD §2 with measured context, mutually exclusive options, a recommendation, a consequence and an effect on budget and schedule. **A recommendation is not a signature.** Requirements depending on an unsigned decision are not buildable, and the pull request carrying this package stays a **draft** until they are answered.

- **Decision 5 — `OD-2R-8` is a real gate, not a formality.** `2P-REMINDER-RECURRENCE` was refused **by name** by the owner in ADR-123's amendment, on budget rather than on desirability. A refusal recorded by the owner is lifted by the owner and by nobody else — least of all by a later phase quietly planning the refused thing. **If it is not lifted, Phase 2R as written has no subject** and `OD-2R-1` must be answered B, C or D.

- **Decision 6 — the automation record is CORRECTED, by appending.** ADR-128 Decision 9, ADR-129 Decision 10 and ADR-130 Decision 8 each state *"All six automation categories stay `suggest_only` with no automatic writer."* **Read live from the deployed database, two of the six are not `suggest_only`:** `task` and `person` are stored as **`automatic_when_eligible`**, created 2026-08-20 at 12:14:34 and 12:14:48 UTC. `project` and `organization` are `suggest_only`; `memory` and `relation` have no row and compute to `suggest_only`.
  - **Attribution, stated before anything is inferred.** The four rows were created within thirty seconds of one another, in ascending order, through the settings surface, on the day Phase 2P closed. The only writer is `public.set_automation_category_policy`, which is `authenticated` and audited. **This is the owner's own action.** It is not an unattributed change and not an agent write, and the defect is in the record alone.
  - **The product is safe, and by a different mechanism than the record names.** Two independent facts hold it: **there is no automatic writer** — `private.automation_category_decision` is consumed only by `public.automation_category_status()`, which is consumed only by a display path, and nothing in `supabase/migrations/`, `src/` or `supabase/functions/` writes as a consequence of `eligible`; and **calibration is empty**, so `eligible` is `false` everywhere. The *"no automatic writer"* half of the sentence is true and is the real protection. The *"all six stay `suggest_only`"* half is false.
  - **Why it is corrected now rather than later.** The owner has **already consented** to automation for two categories. Any future phase that builds an automatic writer must start from the stored rows, not from three ADRs that say otherwise. **No ADR is edited**; this decision is the correction.
  - **Nothing is changed to make the sentence true.** No policy row is rewritten, no state is reset, and no hosted data is touched. Correcting a record by altering the thing it describes is the inversion this repository exists to prevent.

- **Decision 7 — the inherited remainders are classified, not absorbed.** The audit classifies every open item with a destination. `RG-DEP-3` stays a rollout matter and **cannot be closed by writing a file**; push HTTP 403 stays external and hardware, with Android never executed and no device to execute it on; `2P-CHAT-007-JOURNEY` stays **unspendable rather than declined**; `2P-AUTONOMY-005`/`-006`/`-007`/`-008` stay `not-built-by-rule` under ADR-123 Decision 3; `2P-CALENDAR-MONTH-TELEMETRY` stays refused; `2P-MOBILE-002`'s keyboard and IME half stays owner hardware; the four automation review flows stay out under `OD-2Q-8`; the dark accessibility scan on real routes stays a later initiative. **`2P-ACCESS-005` stays NOT EXECUTED — OWNER WAIVED**, is not a priority of this phase, and nothing here may ever be reported as screen-reader evidence.

- **Decision 8 — two proved defects are recorded and routed out.** The audit found, by measurement rather than by reading: `/app/search` reads **no** `searchParams` while ten of the twelve comparable list routes do, so a search cannot be linked, shared, bookmarked or returned to; and *Precisa de você*'s `activeFilter` is component-local state with no URL backing, so back navigation resets it — which is `2P-ATTENTION-008`'s open half, now with a mechanism. **Their disposition is `OD-2R-9`**, recommended as a small separate initiative, because the owner's standing instruction is that the successor must not become a container for the project's debt. **`2P-ATTENTION-008` is not discharged by being understood more precisely.**

- **Decision 9 — the phase-start guard retargets to the roadmap successor.** `src/lib/closeout/phase-2f-documentation.test.ts`'s A13 detector moves off Phase 2R — which this ADR authorizes — and onto the next unauthorized lettered phase, **in this same commit**, so the invariant is never unenforced in between. **The letter is pinned in five places, not one**, and every pin moves here deliberately rather than by a global replace: the detector's three constants; `phase-2o-declarations.test.ts`'s literal pin on the detector's target; `scripts/generate-phase-2p-traceability.mjs` refusal 12; and `scripts/generate-phase-2q-traceability.mjs`'s successor check. `STATE.md` and ADR-130 both recorded the letter as appearing *"only inside the A13 detector"*; **that was wrong**, and it is corrected here by enumeration. **This ADR names no successor**, here or in its heading, because an authorizing ADR that named the phase it was handing the guard to would start that phase in the act of authorizing this one.

- **Decision 10 — one guard is repaired, and not by weakening it.** `phase-2q-declarations.test.ts` asserts that ADR-127 and ADR-128 do not name the successor, over a body taken as `decisions.slice(at)` — **unbounded, running to the end of the file.** Because `DECISIONS.md` is append-only, any later ADR naming the successor would fail an assertion about an earlier one. The slices are **bounded to their own ADR block**, which is the subject each assertion names; the regex is **not** loosened and the assertion is **not** removed. A two-sided control proves the bounded form still fires when that ADR itself names the successor.

- **Decision 11 — nothing changes silently.** Signup stays **closed**. The rollout gate stays **25 pass · 3 fail · 2 owner-signature**. No automation category's state is changed by this package — the two the owner set stay exactly as the owner set them. No grant, RLS policy, retention rule or authority moves. No BYOK credit is spent and **no AI call is made**. No audio is persisted. No hosted data is written: every hosted statement behind this package was a `select`, no fixture was planted, and no residue is claimed because none can exist. **ADR-055 still expires 2026-10-27** — 66 days from this decision — unchanged by this phase and named so it is not discovered late.

- **Alternatives considered.** Plan the successor from the published roadmap (rejected: it ends at Phase 2O and names nothing beyond, so the objective is derived from the product measured in the audit — the same reasoning ADR-126 recorded). Choose autonomy as the theme (rejected: measured, `automation_calibration_observations` holds zero rows and `task` needs 50 at 0.90 precision, so the phase would build the product's most dangerous component with no evidence to validate it and would most likely ship something that never fires). Absorb the two proved defects, the four automation flows and the accessibility infrastructure into one phase (rejected: the owner's instruction is explicit that the successor must not become a container for the project's debt). Fix the automation record by setting the two policy rows back to `suggest_only` (rejected: altering the thing a record describes in order to make the record true is an inversion, and the rows are the owner's own choice). Loosen `phase-2q-declarations.test.ts`'s regex instead of bounding its slice (rejected: the resolution to a guard firing correctly is authorization or a corrected subject, never a weaker pattern — the precedent ADR-126 Decision 5 set).

- **Consequences.** The repository gains a governing artifact for Phase 2R and the A13 detector moves one letter on, through all five of its pins. **Migrations stay 100 local = 100 hosted, parity `202608210100`**, because this package creates none. The estimate is **6.5 / 13.5 / 21.5 working days** with a ~12-day critical path, and the phase is blocked at slice 2R.1 until `OD-2R-1`, `OD-2R-7` and `OD-2R-8` are signed. The automation record is correct for the first time since 2026-08-20. **A start signal for the *new* successor appearing in this repository is a defect** until the owner authorizes one.

## ADR-132 — The owner signs all nine Phase 2R decisions, lifts the reminder-recurrence refusal, and allocates the one migration

- **Date:** 2026-08-23
- **Status:** Accepted — an **owner decision**. It signs the nine decisions ADR-131 left open and **allocates one migration**. **It authorizes no implementation, no migration file, no deployment, no hosted data change, no signup change and no rollout change.** Phase 2R remains **planning only**: implementation still needs a separate owner decision.
- **Context.** ADR-131 authorized Phase 2R for planning and declared `OD-2R-1` … `OD-2R-9` open, each with measured context, mutually exclusive options and a recommendation. The package was then reconciled onto `main` `73f30b39` and every premise revalidated, which moved two hosted facts and corrected one argument without changing the ranking (audit §10). **The owner has now answered all nine, and every one takes its recommendation.** **ADR-131 is not edited** — the rule since ADR-108 — and nothing it decided is reopened.

- **Decision 1 — `OD-2R-8`, option A, and it is recorded first because it is the gate.** The `2P-REMINDER-RECURRENCE` refusal is **LIFTED**, **strictly limited to reminders**. ADR-123's amendment refused recurrence by name, on budget rather than on desirability; a refusal recorded by the owner is lifted by the owner, and it is lifted here. **The lift does not extend to tasks, to the calendar, or to any other object** — Decision 7 is what keeps that boundary. Phase 2R now has its subject.

- **Decision 2 — `OD-2R-1`, option A: the theme is Rotina — o que se repete.** The subject stands as derived: `public.reminders` has twelve columns, none able to express repetition, no `jsonb` column to repurpose, and no `recurrence`, `rrule` or `repeat_*` anywhere in `supabase/migrations/` — re-proved against `main` `73f30b39`. The three alternatives are **declined**, each by name and with its reason on the record: **B (autonomy)** because evidence accrues at two rows per reviewed entry against thresholds of 50 and 80 and four of six categories have no producer at all; **C (find it, and come back to it)** because it is honestly a slice rather than a phase, and it goes to Decision 9 instead; **D (evidence for autonomy)** because it inherits B's rate problem in weaker form and would close with *"nothing is eligible yet"*.

- **Decision 3 — `OD-2R-2`, option A: a closed set of enumerated patterns, and `RRULE` is refused by name.** Daily, weekly on chosen weekdays, monthly on a day-of-month, monthly on an ordinal weekday, yearly — stored as a validated, versioned JSON object. **No RFC 5545 parser is written and no `RRULE` string is stored or accepted.** The reason is the failure mode, not the capability: an `RRULE` the parser accepts and interprets differently from the writer produces reminders at wrong times **with no error anywhere**, while a closed set is validated at the boundary and is exhaustively testable.

- **Decision 4 — `OD-2R-3`, option A: exactly one materialised occurrence at a time.** The series holds the rule; exactly one concrete `reminders` row exists for the next occurrence, and completing it materialises the following one, **idempotently in the database rather than in the caller**. This is what keeps the hourly heartbeat, its per-user lock, quiet hours, the daily cap and the 24-hour cooldown **unchanged rather than re-implemented** — they already operate on `reminders` rows and will keep operating on exactly one. A materialised horizon and a pure compute-on-read are both **declined**.

- **Decision 5 — `OD-2R-4`, option A: every edit asks, and the default is the narrower.** The control offers *this occurrence* and *this and future*, and **defaults to *this occurrence***. A control whose default silently changes every future occurrence is the shape of an irreversible surprise. A detached occurrence stays detached, and the scope actually applied is reported back after the edit.

- **Decision 6 — `OD-2R-5`, option A: a recurrence is a wall-clock intention, and the three unrepresentable cases are decided now rather than discovered.** *"09:00 every weekday"* means 09:00 local before and after a daylight-saving transition, and the absolute instant moves. The owner signs all three edge cases explicitly:
  1. a local time that **does not exist** on a spring-forward day **advances to the first valid local instant**;
  2. a local time that **occurs twice** on a fall-back day takes the **first** occurrence;
  3. a day-of-month that **does not exist** in a given month — the 31st of a 30-day month — **uses the last day of that month**.
  Each is pinned by a test that fails if the behaviour changes. This is the decision most likely to be got wrong, and this repository has been wrong here before: three local-day implementations once coexisted, two wrong in opposite directions.

- **Decision 7 — `OD-2R-6`, option A: recurring tasks are explicitly OUT of Phase 2R.** Tasks carry a confirmation lifecycle, dependencies, projects, people and an undo-compensation contract, every one of which would acquire a recurrence dimension. Priced at **+6 to +9 days and at least one further migration** — a second phase in disguise. It becomes the named remainder **`2R-TASK-RECURRENCE`**, with the owner as its destination. **Decision 1's lift does not reach it.**

- **Decision 8 — `OD-2R-7`, option A: exactly one migration is ALLOCATED, and its destination is exclusive.** It exists **solely** to persist the recurrence model — `OD-2R-2`'s shape and the series-to-occurrence relation — and nothing else. **This ADR allocates it; it does not create it.** No migration file exists and none may exist until implementation is separately authorized. **A second migration of any kind is a stop condition** that halts the phase and returns to the owner. A budget whose ceiling is not also its stop condition is not a budget.

- **Decision 9 — `OD-2R-9`, option A: the two proved defects are a separate small initiative.** A search that cannot be linked, shared, bookmarked or returned to (`/app/search` reads no `searchParams` while ten of the twelve comparable list routes do), and *Precisa de você*'s filter lost on back navigation (`2P-ATTENTION-008`'s open half, mechanism proved) both leave Phase 2R. **Neither is discharged by being routed**, and `2P-ATTENTION-008` is not discharged by being understood more precisely.

- **Decision 10 — what these signatures do not authorize.** Implementation is **not** authorized: no slice may start, no product code may be written, **no migration file may be created**, nothing is deployed, and no hosted data is written. Signing the decisions that would deliver a feature is not delivering it, and it is not authorizing its construction either. The pull request carrying this package may leave **draft** — the owner has asked for it to be marked ready for review — and **must not be merged** without a separate authorization.

- **Decision 11 — the out-of-phase items stay out, each with a destination.** `2R-TASK-RECURRENCE` (Decision 7); the two proved defects (Decision 9); **the dead-man switch blind to a gateway 401** — operations, repaired by alerting on staleness of `last_success_at` rather than on a failure count, and named so that excluding it is a decision rather than an omission; the dark accessibility scan on real routes; push HTTP 403 and Android; `RG-DEP-3`, which **cannot be closed by writing a file**; `2P-CHAT-007-JOURNEY`, unspendable; the four automation review flows under `OD-2Q-8`; and **autonomy, which stays out until calibration evidence exists** rather than because it is unwanted. **`2P-ACCESS-005` stays NOT EXECUTED — OWNER WAIVED** and nothing in this phase may ever be reported as screen-reader evidence.

- **Decision 12 — nothing else moves.** Signup stays **closed**. The rollout gate stays **25 pass · 3 fail · 2 owner-signature**. All six automation categories stay `suggest_only` — which, since the owner undid the 2026-08-20 opt-in, is again what the deployed rows say (audit §10.3); **ADR-131 Decision 6 is not edited and is superseded there by name**. No grant, RLS policy, retention rule or authority moves. No BYOK credit is spent and **no AI call is made**. No audio is persisted. **ADR-055 still expires 2026-10-27.** **The phase after this one is not started, not planned and not named as active**, and the phase-start guard stays where ADR-131 Decision 9 put it.

- **Alternatives considered.** Sign only the gate and return for the rest (rejected: `OD-2R-2` … `OD-2R-5` each change the estimate, and a package half-signed cannot be estimated). Allocate two migrations pre-emptively so occurrence state has somewhere to go (rejected: the need is not proved, and `OD-2R-3` option A is specifically the shape that does not need a second table). Take `OD-2R-2` option B for expressiveness (rejected: the owner's reason is the silent failure mode, and no personal reminder needs a pattern the closed set cannot express). Fold `OD-2R-9`'s two defects in because they are small (rejected: the standing instruction is that the successor must not become a container for the project's debt, and "small" is how a container starts).

- **Consequences.** Phase 2R moves from **planned with nine decisions open** to **planned and fully signed**, still **not authorized for implementation**. The migration budget becomes **1 ALLOCATED · 0 spent**, destination exclusive, second a stop condition. **The requirement count does not move: 73 across ten families**, because every signature took the option the package was written for — no requirement is added, renumbered, reused or removed. The estimate stands at **6.5 / 13.5 / 21.5** working days with a ~12-day critical path, and slice 2R.1 is **no longer blocked by a decision** — it is blocked only by the missing implementation authorization. `2R-TASK-RECURRENCE` is created as a named remainder. **A start signal for the roadmap successor appearing in this repository remains a defect** until the owner authorizes one.

## ADR-133 — The owner authorizes the implementation of Phase 2R, from slice 2R.0 through the closing checkpoint, and spends the one allocated migration

- **Date:** 2026-08-23
- **Status:** Accepted — an **owner decision**. It authorizes the **construction** of what ADR-131 planned and ADR-132 signed: slices **2R.0 … 2R.5**, branches, code, tests, guards, documentation and acceptance records, one pull request per slice, each marked ready only after its gates, merged only on green CI at the pull request head, and verified again on the **exact merge SHA**. It authorizes the creation and hosted application of **the one migration ADR-132 allocated**, the deploys and hosted proofs strictly necessary to prove it, and **only** synthetic, owner-scoped fixtures that are removed with a two-sided residue control. **It does not authorize the phase to close:** closure requires the owner's validation checkpoint and a separate ADR.
- **Context.** ADR-131 authorized planning; ADR-132 signed all nine decisions and allocated one migration; both stated, in terms, that **signing is not authorizing implementation** and that **allocated is not created**. Implementation plan §4 named the missing implementation authorization as *"the only blocker on the whole phase"*. This ADR is that authorization and nothing more. **ADR-131 and ADR-132 are not edited** — the rule since ADR-108 — and **not one of ADR-132's twelve decisions is reopened, narrowed or widened here.**

- **Decision 1 — the six slices are authorized to be built, in order.** 2R.0 measure · 2R.1 the model and the one migration · 2R.2 occurrence versus series with undo · 2R.3 the surface · 2R.4 delivery · 2R.5 closeout preparation. Each is **re-audited against the `main` its predecessor produced**, not against the plan. Each ships as **one pull request**, including documentation: Phase 2P's deviation of pushing docs straight to `main` is not repeated.

- **Decision 2 — the gates are the authorization, not a description of it.** A slice's pull request is marked ready only after lint, typecheck, the unit suite, the production build and the slice's own journeys; it is merged only on green CI at its head; and **CI is verified again on the exact merge SHA**, which is a different fact from a green pull-request head and is never reported as one. A slice whose gates did not run is recorded as not run.

- **Decision 3 — the one allocated migration may be created, and only after its whole gate chain.** Migration and pgTAP complete; database lint; the entire chain applied to an empty database in CI; positive, negative and non-vacuity controls; green CI at the pull request head; merge; green CI on the exact merge SHA; **proof that the bytes to be applied are byte-identical to the bytes on `main`**; a hosted migration list read *before* the application; a dry run showing **exactly one** pending migration; the application; an owner-scoped hosted proof with its cleanup; and a fresh read showing local = remote with parity advanced by **exactly one**. **A second migration of any kind remains a stop condition** that halts the phase and returns to the owner. ADR-132 Decision 8 is unchanged: this ADR spends the allocation, it does not enlarge it.

- **Decision 4 — hosted data may be touched only as a fixture, and only two-sidedly.** Fixtures are **synthetic, owner-scoped and removed**. Residue is proved by a two-sided control — plant a row, prove the probe sees it, remove it, prove the probe no longer does. A zero count over an empty table is not a control and is not accepted as one.

- **Decision 5 — what this authorization explicitly does not permit.** A second migration; relocating the allocated one; recurrence on **tasks** (ADR-132 Decision 7 stands, remainder `2R-TASK-RECURRENCE`); `RRULE` or any open recurrence language (ADR-132 Decision 3 stands, and `RRULE` stays **refused by name**); any change to the heartbeat, quiet hours, the daily cap or the 24-hour cooldown; resuming or repairing the push HTTP 403; spending a BYOK credential or making **any** AI call; opening signup; moving the rollout gate; starting or planning the roadmap successor; committing to `main`; force-pushing; deleting a branch or a worktree; permanent work outside a pull request; and **declaring hardware proof from an emulator, a viewport, a document or an automated test**.

- **Decision 6 — the phase stops at the owner's validation, not at a green pipeline.** `2R-CLOSE-012` is unchanged. Slice 2R.5 prepares the closing report and the device checklist and **does not declare the phase complete**. The formal closeout is a separate owner decision recorded as its own ADR, after the checkpoint. **VoiceOver is not required of that checkpoint**: `2P-ACCESS-005` stays **NOT EXECUTED — OWNER WAIVED**, is recorded honestly as not executed, and never blocks the checkpoint and never appears as evidence.

- **Decision 7 — nothing else moves.** Signup stays **closed**. The rollout gate stays **25 pass · 3 fail · 2 owner-signature**, and `RG-DEP-3` **cannot be closed by writing a file**. No grant, RLS policy, retention rule or authority moves beyond what the one migration's exclusive destination requires. All six automation categories stay `suggest_only`. No audio is persisted. **ADR-055 still expires 2026-10-27.** **The phase after this one is not started, not planned and not named as active**, and the phase-start guard stays where ADR-131 Decision 9 put it.

- **Alternatives considered.** Authorize slice 2R.0 alone and return for each subsequent slice (rejected: 2R.0 changes no behaviour, so a per-slice authorization would spend the owner's attention on the one slice that cannot go wrong, and would stall at the slices that can). Authorize implementation and closure together (rejected: `2R-CLOSE-012` exists because every gate green is when a wrong claim is easiest, and the owner's device is the only thing that can answer `2R-MOBILE-003`). Pre-authorize a second migration in case occurrence state needs one (rejected: ADR-132 Decision 8 made the ceiling the stop condition deliberately, and pre-authorizing the thing that would signal a design error removes the signal). Fold `OD-2R-9`'s two proved defects in, since implementation is now open anyway (rejected: the standing instruction is that the successor must not become a container for the project's debt, and an authorization is not a re-scoping).

- **Consequences.** Phase 2R moves from **planned and fully signed** to **authorized for implementation and not closed**. The migration budget becomes **1 allocated · 0 spent · 0 created**, and slice 2R.1 is the only slice permitted to change any of those three numbers. The declaration guard's *"nothing is implemented"* assertions are **inverted slice by slice rather than deleted**, because an absence nobody asserts is an absence nobody notices disappearing. **The requirement count does not move: 73 across ten families**, because an authorization to build is not a change of scope. A start signal for the roadmap successor appearing in this repository **remains a defect**.

## ADR-134 — The owner answers slice 2R.0's stop condition: the recurrence model uses the timezone contract, and the second authority is routed out with a destination

- **Date:** 2026-08-23
- **Status:** Accepted — an **owner decision**, taken on the stop condition slice 2R.0 reached and reported. It **unblocks slice 2R.1** and authorizes nothing else: no additional migration, no widening of Phase 2R's scope, and no change to any decision ADR-132 signed or any limit ADR-133 set.
- **Context.** `2R-FOUNDATION-004` required the owner's timezone-resolution path to be named and **any second path reported as a defect**. Slice 2R.0 found one and reported it rather than building on either: `resolveOwnerTimeZone` is the contract — a value must construct an `Intl.DateTimeFormat` **and** contain `/` or be exactly `UTC`, else the declared default — while **eight inline call sites across seven files** keep any non-empty string. Two of the eight are `app/reminders/page.tsx` and `features/reminders/actions.ts`, which is the surface this phase builds a wall-clock feature onto. The divergence is **reachable, not theoretical**: `profiles.timezone` carries **no check constraint** (read live from `pg_constraint`) and `public.save_profile_and_preferences` stores the submitted value unvalidated, so the only enforcement is a Zod refinement in a Server Action. A bare abbreviation such as `EST` constructs happily and carries **no daylight-saving rule**. No stored value diverges today — both profiles are `America/Sao_Paulo` — and the two rules agree on that value exactly.

- **Decision 1 — option A: the recurrence model resolves every instant through the contract, and the eight pre-existing sites are left exactly as they are.** `private.reminder_owner_timezone` applies the contract's rule and falls back to the same declared default, and `recurrence-rule-parity.test.ts` asserts the SQL fallback equals `defaultAgentPreferences.timezone` so the pair cannot drift. That satisfies `2R-TIME-005` for **everything this phase builds**, at zero added days, without the phase widening into repairing eight call sites it did not create.

- **Decision 2 — `2R-TZ-SECOND-AUTHORITY` is a named remainder with a destination, and is NOT discharged by being understood.** Its destination is the **same separate small initiative** `OD-2R-9` routed the two proved defects to. The census is kept executable in `phase-2r-foundation.test.ts` rather than in prose, so a ninth site added without a decision fails, and so does one repaired without the record being updated. **No document in Phase 2R may report it as closed.**

- **Decision 3 — the missing check constraint on `profiles.timezone` is routed out, not folded in.** It is a real gap: the database accepts what the Server Action refuses. Repairing it needs a **migration**, and Phase 2R has exactly one whose destination is exclusive to the recurrence model. Taking a second would be the stop condition `OD-2R-7` exists to make visible. It goes to the same destination as Decision 2, and this ADR is the record that excluding it was a decision rather than an omission.

- **Alternatives considered.** Repair all eight sites inside Phase 2R (rejected: +0.5 to +1 day, touching `chat`, `history`, `work` and `daily-cycle` — four surfaces with no relationship to recurrence, four that would need their own journeys, and precisely the debt-container the standing instruction forbids). Halt Phase 2R until the second authority is repaired under its own initiative (rejected: it spends the phase's whole ~12-day critical path to prevent a divergence Decision 1 already prevents, on data where no stored value diverges). Add the check constraint inside the allocated migration since it is "one line" (rejected: the allocation's destination is exclusive by signature, and "it is only one line" is how an exclusive destination stops being one).

- **Consequences.** Slice 2R.1 proceeds. The recurrence engine reads the contract's rule and nothing else, and the two implementations of that rule — one in TypeScript for display, one in SQL for instants — are held identical by an executable parity test rather than by intention. The eight inline sites remain exactly as correct as they were before this phase started, named, censused and owned. **A phase that had silently used the laxer rule would have passed every gate**, which is what makes the report worth more than the repair.

## ADR-135 — The owner closes Phase 2R after approving the final checkpoint on real hardware

- **Date:** 2026-08-24
- **Status:** Accepted — an **owner decision**, taken after the phase's closing device checkpoint was run on the owner's own iPhone. It closes Phase 2R and authorizes nothing else: **no migration, no deploy, no BYOK, no push work, no remainder repair, and no successor phase.**

- **Context.** `2R-CLOSE-012` requires closure to be an owner decision recorded as an ADR **after a device checkpoint**, and says in terms that a green pipeline is not that. Every gate had been green for some time; none of them could close the phase, which is the point of the requirement.

- **Decision 1 — the final checkpoint is APPROVED, on real hardware.** The owner validated, on their own iPhone: daily recurrence; **weekly with Monday, Wednesday and Friday in a single series**; monthly recurrence; the preview before saving; **one live occurrence per active series**; *somente esta*; *esta e as futuras*; cancelling one occurrence without cancelling the series; ending a series while preserving history; the undo the series offers; presentation on the list, the calendar and the agenda; and the absence of zoom, of the page scrolling behind an open modal, and of content loss.

- **Decision 2 — Phase 2R is CLOSED.** *Rotina: o que se repete* is delivered. **73 requirements declared · 73 classified · zero unclassified**, from a generated matrix that refuses rather than emitting a partial one: **51 `built` · 17 `baseline` · 2 `partial` · 3 `not-built-by-rule` · 0 `undelivered`**. A non-zero `undelivered` count would have been a phase failure rather than a category, and the count is zero.

- **Decision 3 — the migration budget closes where it opened: one allocated, one spent, one applied.** `202608230101`, parity `202608230101`, **101 local = 101 hosted**, re-read live at closeout. Slices 2R.2, 2R.3, 2R.4, 2R.5 and **both** corrective rounds inside 2R.3 created none between them. `OD-2R-7`'s exclusive destination held for the whole phase.

- **Decision 4 — `T-2R-11` and `T-2R-12` receive their disposition NOW, and could not have received it earlier.** Ten of the twelve threats closed at slice 2R.5. These two were carried, deliberately, because **both are about the act of closing the phase** — the successor starting by accident, and a hardware proof discharged by a document. Disposing of them before this ADR existed would have been the exact substitution `T-2R-12` describes. They close here, on the same evidence that closes the phase: a person with the device, and this record.

- **Decision 5 — every remainder is carried out of the phase intact, and none is discharged by closure.** `2R-TZ-SECOND-AUTHORITY` (used by slice 2R.4 to force a real failure, which is not fixing it); `2R-UNDO-LEDGER-NOT-CLOSED`; `2R-OCCURRENCE-CANCEL-IRREVERSIBLE`; `2R-AXE-MANUAL-LANE`; `2R-RECURRENCE-LANE-UNRUNNABLE`; `2R-DRAWER-NOT-LOCKED`; `2R-TASK-RECURRENCE`; `OD-2R-9`'s two defects; the interval gap; push HTTP 403, now guarded against being *claimed*. Inherited and reproduced with **no item dropped**: `2P-ACCESS-005` **NOT EXECUTED — OWNER WAIVED**, `2P-ATTENTION-008`, `RG-DEP-3`, `2P-CHAT-007-JOURNEY`, ADR-055 expiring **2026-10-27**. **Closing a phase does not close what it carried.**

- **Decision 6 — Phase 2S is NOT started, NOT planned and NOT named as active.** This ADR authorizes no successor. The phase-start detector, the generator's successor refusal and the declaration guard's forbidden successor directories all remain in force, and a read-only re-audit is the only thing permitted before a separate owner authorization.

- **Alternatives considered.** Close on the green pipeline once every gate passed (rejected by `2R-CLOSE-012` itself, and the phase's own history is the argument: **the second device checkpoint found three defects under a fully green pipeline**, one of which would have written one weekday where the modal displayed three). Dispose of `T-2R-11` and `T-2R-12` at slice 2R.5 with the other ten (rejected: closing a threat about the act of closing, before closing, is the substitution the threat names). Discharge the smaller remainders as part of closeout since the phase was already open (rejected: a closeout that repairs is a closeout whose scope nobody bounded, and each remainder has a recorded destination).

- **Consequences.** Phase 2R is closed and the product can express a rhythm: reminders repeat daily, weekly on any set of weekdays, monthly by date or ordinal weekday, and yearly — with **exactly one live occurrence per active series**, materialised by a trigger fired by a completion rather than by a scheduler. The hourly heartbeat, its per-user lock, quiet hours, the daily cap and the 24-hour cooldown are **unchanged rather than re-implemented**, which is `OD-2R-3` option A's whole value and is now proved by execution rather than by reading the source.

  Three findings outlive the phase. **Postgres's own `AT TIME ZONE` disagrees with two of the three signed daylight-saving cases, in opposite directions** — measured before the migration was written, and a migration built on the native operator would have fired at the wrong time at every transition with no error anywhere. **A defect reported as visual was a wrong write**: the modal showed three weekdays, previewed three, and would have saved one. **Five requirements had taken credit for work nobody did** — the contract had said since planning that `baseline` may never be recorded as `built`, and nothing read it until the closeout generator compared delivered classes against declared kinds; the misfiling survived five slices, three device checkpoints and every green CI run.

  The last of those is the one worth carrying forward as a working rule: **a contract stated in prose is not a contract anybody enforces.**

## ADR-136 — The owner authorizes Phase 2S planning, corrects three facts the record had wrong, and retargets the phase-start guard to the roadmap successor

- **Date:** 2026-08-24
- **Status:** Accepted — an **owner decision**. It authorizes **planning only**: a PRD, an implementation plan, a theme comparison, an audit, a gaps report, a threat model, a traceability contract, a requirement coverage report, and the documentary guards that keep them fail-closed. **It authorizes no implementation, no slice, no migration, no deployment, no hosted write, no signup change, no rollout change, no resumption of the push HTTP 403 investigation, no restore drill, no BYOK spend, no AI call, no remainder execution, and no successor phase.**

- **Context.** ADR-135 closed Phase 2R and its Decision 6 recorded that the successor was *"NOT started, NOT planned and NOT named as active"*, permitting only a read-only re-audit before a separate owner authorization. This is that authorization. **ADR-131 through ADR-135 are not edited** — the rule since ADR-108 is that an accepted ADR in this series is never rewritten into agreement with a later one. Each stands as signed, and the three factual corrections this ADR carries are made **by appending**, exactly as ADR-129 corrected ADR-127's premise and ADR-131 corrected the automation record.

- **Decision 1 — Phase 2S is authorized for PLANNING.** Its subject is derived from a measured census of the product, not from a roadmap: `PHASE_2K_2O_ROADMAP_DESIGN.md` **ends at Phase 2O** and names nothing beyond it, so there is no roadmap definition to inherit. The governing pair is `docs/initiatives/phase-2s/PHASE_2S_PRD.md` and `PHASE_2S_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md`; the evidence lives in `docs/reports/phase-2s/`. **74 requirements across ten families and five slices, none implemented and none classified.**

- **Decision 2 — the theme is CHOSEN, and it is the only thing here that is.** The owner chose *Responder ao Brain* — the notification and attention loop — on 2026-08-24, from four costed options in `PHASE_2S_THEME_OPTIONS.md`. The choice rests on measurement rather than preference: **57 notifications, 54 of them `task_stale`, 0 read, 0 dismissed, exactly 3 per day for 18 unbroken days, about 3 tasks last touched on 2026-07-30** — and **marking a notification read suppresses nothing**, because the heartbeat's suppression window never reads `status` and the dedupe key carries the local date. The three alternatives lost on measured grounds: chat and citations because the corpus is **one entry and one memory**; recurring tasks because **zero tasks have ever reached `completed`**; continuity because Phase 2R's own audit already classified it *"a slice, not a phase"*. **Choosing the theme is not signing the package.**

- **Decision 3 — ten decisions are OPEN, and this ADR signs none of them.** `OD-2S-1` … `OD-2S-10` are declared in PRD §2 with measured context, mutually exclusive options, a recommendation, a consequence and an effect on budget and schedule. **A recommendation is not a signature.** Requirements depending on an unsigned decision are not buildable, and the pull request carrying this package stays a **draft** until they are answered. `OD-2S-1`, `OD-2S-4`, `OD-2S-5` and `OD-2S-9` **add or strike requirements** rather than amending them, so the requirement set is provisional until they are answered.

- **Decision 4 — the migration budget is PROPOSED, not signed.** The package proposes **exactly one**, whose destination is the suppression state, the cadence rule and the notice's destination. Necessity is proved rather than asserted, read live: `public.notifications` holds **eleven** columns and `public.tasks` **twenty-three**, **neither has a `jsonb` column**, and no `suppress`, `mute` or `nudge` vocabulary exists anywhere in `supabase/migrations/`. Whatever `OD-2S-1` is answered, `run_user_heartbeat` changes, and in this repository a function change **is** a migration. **This ADR allocates none.** Allocation is `OD-2S-7` and requires a separate owner signature. **A second migration of any kind is a stop condition.**

- **Decision 5 — the phase's principal risk is named in the authorization, not discovered in a slice.** `OD-2S-9` proposes changing `run_user_heartbeat`, which every phase since 2M has deliberately left alone. Every rule it already enforces — quiet hours, the daily cap, the 24-hour cooldown, the per-user lock, and one user's failure not blocking the batch — therefore becomes a `baseline` requirement that must be **re-proved by execution**. Slice 2R.1 matched two substrings against `pg_proc.prosrc` and proved nothing about behaviour; slice 2R.4 called `run_user_heartbeat` twenty-six times and found a real defect in its own assertion. **Reading the source is not a proof and may not be recorded as one.**

- **Decision 6 — `2P-CHAT-007-JOURNEY` is no longer "unspendable", and the record is corrected by appending.** Phases 2P, 2Q and 2R each recorded it as *"unspendable — it needs the owner's own credential."* Read live from the deployed database: `public.user_ai_credentials` holds **one row** — `provider = openai`, `status = active`, `validated_at = 2026-08-02`, `last_failure_code = null`. **The credential exists and is active, and has for twenty-two days.** The remainder is **not discharged by being reclassified**; it stays out of this phase, now correctly described. **No ADR is edited**, and nothing is changed to make the old sentence true.

- **Decision 7 — voice with an editable transcript is BUILT and has never once run, and this changes what is owed rather than what is built.** The owner named *captura por voz com transcrição editável antes de enviar ao Brain* as a future priority. Measured: `src/features/capture/voice-composer.tsx` ships and is **mounted** at `src/features/capture/composer.tsx:575`; its delivered contract is *record → transcribe → **the composer's editable field** → type more → submit*, inserting at the caret. `capture_mode_selected` with `captureMode: "voice"` fired **once**, on 2026-08-22, and `public.ai_usage_events` holds **zero** `transcription` rows against a vocabulary that allows the operation. **What is owed is a proof on the owner's device — or a defect report if it fails — not a build.** It is named in PRD §7.1 with that destination and is **not** this phase's scope.

- **Decision 8 — a guard defect is corrected in the same commit that retargets it.** The A13 detector's declared-requirement signal is `/^- \*\*2S-[A-Z]+-\d{3}/m` — a **bullet**. This repository declares requirements in **PRD tables**, and there is **not one bullet-shaped declaration anywhere under `docs/`**, so that signal has never once matched a real declaration; its control tests the pattern against a planted string of a shape nothing produces. **A13 as a whole still fired**, through the governing-artifact filename and the accepted-ADR heading, so no phase could have started silently through it. The constant now matches **both** shapes, with a two-sided control that plants a table row and proves the old pattern would have missed it. **A retarget that carried a dead signal forward would be the same failure Phase 2R named: a contract stated in prose is not a contract anybody enforces.** The detector's fourth signal is **narrower than it reads** — its directory scan is not recursive — and is **recorded rather than widened**, because widening a second signal inside a retarget is more change than this authorization covers.

- **Decision 9 — the phase-start guard retargets to the roadmap successor, through every one of its SIX pins.** `src/lib/closeout/phase-2f-documentation.test.ts`'s A13 detector moves off Phase 2S — which this ADR authorizes — and onto the next unauthorized lettered phase, **in this same commit**, so the invariant is never unenforced in between. **The letter is pinned in six places, and every pin moves here deliberately rather than by a global replace:** the detector's three constants and its fixture root; `phase-2o-declarations.test.ts`'s literal pins; `phase-2r-declarations.test.ts`'s literal pins, its own copy of the detector family pattern, and its absent-directory assertions; `scripts/generate-phase-2p-traceability.mjs` refusal 12; `scripts/generate-phase-2q-traceability.mjs`'s successor check; and `scripts/generate-phase-2r-traceability.mjs` refusal 14. **ADR-131 Decision 9 recorded five, and five was right when it was written** — Phase 2R had not yet shipped a generator of its own, and its refusal 14 is the sixth. The count is stated here because ADR-131 corrected an earlier record that said the letter lived *"only inside the A13 detector"*, and a count that silently drifts is the same defect one generation on. **This ADR names no successor**, here or in its heading, because an authorizing ADR that named the phase it was handing the guard to would start that phase in the act of authorizing this one.

- **Decision 10 — the inherited remainders are classified, not absorbed.** Every open item is reproduced in PRD §7.1 with a destination. `RG-DEP-3` stays a rollout matter and **cannot be closed by writing a file**; push HTTP 403 stays external, `notification_deliveries` reads **zero**, and `2S-TRUST-008` guards the phase against *claiming* otherwise; `2P-ACCESS-005` stays **NOT EXECUTED — OWNER WAIVED**, is not a priority of this phase, and nothing here may ever be reported as screen-reader evidence; `2R-DRAWER-NOT-LOCKED` stays an **owner design decision**; `2R-TZ-SECOND-AUTHORITY`, `2R-UNDO-LEDGER-NOT-CLOSED`, `2R-OCCURRENCE-CANCEL-IRREVERSIBLE`, `2R-AXE-MANUAL-LANE`, `2R-RECURRENCE-LANE-UNRUNNABLE`, `2R-TASK-RECURRENCE`, `OD-2R-9`'s two defects, the interval gap, `2P-ATTENTION-008`, `2P-REVIEW-CITATIONS` and `2P-MOBILE-002`'s keyboard half all stay open with their destinations unchanged. **ADR-055 expires 2026-10-27** — **64 days** from this decision — and its spike tier needs 50 qualifying commands against `task_command_applied`'s **2**, so **the threshold will not be met**; `OD-2S-10` recommends it stays out, and it is named here because nothing in this repository fires on a date.

- **Decision 11 — one divergence in Phase 2R's closed record is reported and NOT corrected.** `PHASE_2R_THREAT_MODEL.md` ends at slice 2R.5's disposition — ten CLOSED, `T-2R-11` and `T-2R-12` recorded as *"OPEN — carried"*. Their final disposition exists only in ADR-135 Decision 4 and in `STATE.md`. *Twelve of twelve disposed* is **true**; a reader of the threat model alone would conclude ten of twelve. **This authorization covers the Phase 2S package and its own guards, not edits to Phase 2R's closed record**, so the divergence is recorded in the audit and left for a separate decision. Naming it is the point; fixing it here would be scope nobody bounded.

- **Decision 12 — nothing changes silently.** Signup stays **closed**. The rollout gate stays **25 pass · 3 fail · 2 owner-signature**, re-verified by running `npm run rollout:verify` rather than by reading a document. No automation category's state is changed — `automation_category_policies` was re-read live and holds **zero** rows, so all six read through the computed default, and **the instruction that came out of Phase 2R's finding stands: read the rows, never a document, including this one.** No grant, RLS policy, retention rule or authority moves. No BYOK credit is spent and **no AI call is made**, which is a live refusal rather than a vacuous one now that the credential is active. No audio is persisted. **No hosted data is written: every hosted statement behind this package was a `select`, no fixture was planted, and no residue is claimed because none can exist.**

- **Alternatives considered.** Plan the successor from the published roadmap (rejected: it ends at Phase 2O and names nothing beyond, so the objective is derived from the product measured in the audit — the reasoning ADR-126 and ADR-131 both recorded). Choose chat and review citations (rejected: measured, `conversations` and `summaries` both hold **zero** rows and the corpus is one entry and one memory, so a correctly grounded agent would answer *"I don't know"* and the phase would close on a thin honest sentence — the same disqualification ADR-131 applied to autonomy). Choose recurring tasks (rejected: **zero** tasks have ever reached `completed`, so recurrence would repeat a completion that has never once happened, and `OD-2R-6` prices it at +6 to +9 days and a further migration). Choose continuity (rejected: Phase 2R's audit already classified it *"a slice, not a phase"*, and with one entry there is almost nothing to search for). Fold `OD-2R-9`'s two defects in because slice 2S.3 will have one of the files open (**not rejected — deferred to `OD-2S-8`**, because it is a genuinely close call and the owner's to make). Correct the Phase 2R threat model here (rejected: this authorization does not cover edits to a closed phase's record). Retarget the guard by a global replace (rejected: the pins differ in shape — three are literal source assertions, two are directory checks and one is a regular expression inside a generator — and ADR-131 recorded a count that has since grown by one, which a replace would have hidden rather than surfaced).

- **Consequences.** The repository gains a governing artifact for Phase 2S and the A13 detector moves one letter on, through all six of its pins, with one of its signals repaired rather than merely moved. **Migrations stay 101 local = 101 hosted, parity `202608230101`**, because this package creates none. The estimate is **6 / 11.5 / 18.5 working days** with a ~10.5-day critical path and **no parallelisable slices**, and the phase is blocked on two gates in order: the ten signatures, and then a separate implementation authorization. Phase 2R stays **CLOSED**; ADR-135 is not reopened, amended or reinterpreted. Three facts the record had wrong are now right: the BYOK credential is active, voice is built and unproven, and A13's second signal matches the shape this repository actually writes. **A start signal for the *new* successor appearing in this repository is a defect** until the owner authorizes one.
